Conclusion: QICK Box 适用于高保真量子控制与测量应用；其固件与软件方面的进展将在另一篇论文中描述。

Abstract: The QICK (Quantum Instrumentation Control Kit), first introduced in 2022, is a standalone open source control system for quantum information science. Since then, QICK has been migrated to new hardware platforms, expanded its functionality for applications with different types of qubits (superconducting, atomic, ion-trapped, and spin defects), as well as quantum sensors, and quantum networks. QICK is a highly optimized control kit, with a library of functions that can be used for more than one application. In particular, the software and firmware are customized to minimize latency and maximize control performance and throughput. In this white paper, we describe hardware details and performance of the QICK Box. The QICK Box features up to 16 outputs, up to 8 inputs, and 8 20-bit DC biases. Modular output and input daughtercards give the choice of signal chains optimized for fast DC-coupled signals or RF signals up to 10 GHz. Power levels, step attenuation, and bandpass filtering are controlled by software. We demonstrate the system's performance through single-shot readout measurements of a six-transmon device at the Quantum Underground Instrumentation Experimental Testbed (QUIET) at Fermilab, achieving (91.7 +/- 0.4)% readout fidelity limited by T1 decay during the readout. The single shot readout signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) is 4.4 +/- 0.1 without a quantum amplifier, confirming that the QICK Box is suitable for high-fidelity quantum control and measurement applications. Advances in firmware and software will be described in a separate paper.

</details>


### [91] [Symmetry-preserving quantum compilation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03624)
*Maryam Mudassar,Zhi-Yuan Wei,Alexander Schuckert,Michael J. Lawler,Michael J. Gullans,Daniel Gottesman*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作提出了精确保持SU(2)对称性的容错编译框架，通过测量与前馈规避数论障碍，将编译误差限制在对称算子代数内，并在Heisenberg模型输运模拟中显著优于标准Clifford+T合成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 容错量子模拟需将保对称性的时间演化编译为离散门集，但标准Clifford+T合成即使目标演化保持连续对称性也可能破坏该对称性，导致编译后的模拟丢失原系统的本质特征。因此需要一种能精确保持对称性的编译方法。

Method: 作者构建了精确保持SU(2)对称性的容错编译框架：刻画了阻止保对称性Clifford+T幺正电路逼近任意SU(2)对称演化的数论障碍，并利用测量与前馈（measurement and feed-forward）绕过该障碍，使合成误差被限制在保对称算子代数内，而不引入破坏对称性的微扰。

Result: 在一维Heisenberg模型输运模拟中，于匹配的较低T-count（如每两格点Trotter步54个T门）下，标准合成给出错误的输运指数，而所提保对称编译能重现预期指数；若以使该指数达到相同精度所需资源计，该构造将T-count降低约5倍。框架进一步扩展到格点规范理论的规范对称性，以及量子化学和Fermi-Hubbard模型中相关的粒子数与自旋对称性。

Conclusion: 结果表明，保对称性的容错编译不仅能定性改变合成误差的影响，还能大幅降低量子模拟所需资源。

Abstract: Fault-tolerant quantum simulation requires compiling symmetry-preserving time evolutions into discrete gate sets. Standard Clifford+T synthesis can break continuous symmetries even when the target evolution preserves them, causing compiled simulations to lose essential features of the original system. We develop a fault-tolerant compilation framework that preserves SU(2) symmetry exactly. We characterize the number-theoretic obstruction preventing symmetry-preserving unitary Clifford+T circuits from approximating arbitrary SU(2)-symmetric evolutions and circumvent it using measurement and feed-forward. The resulting synthesis confines compilation errors to the symmetry-preserving operator algebra rather than introducing symmetry-breaking perturbations.
  We demonstrate the algorithm by simulating transport in the one-dimensional Heisenberg model. At a lower matched T-count, for instance, 54 T gates per two-site Trotter step, standard synthesis yields an incorrect transport exponent, whereas our symmetry-preserving compilation reproduces the expected exponent for the same expected T-count. If instead each approach is allocated whatever resources are necessary to estimate this exponent to the same accuracy, our construction reduces the T-count by approximately a factor of five. We further extend the framework to gauge symmetries in lattice gauge theories and to particle-number and spin symmetries in fermionic models relevant to quantum chemistry and the Fermi-Hubbard model.
  These results show that symmetry-preserving fault-tolerant compilation can qualitatively change the effects of synthesis error while substantially reducing the resources required for quantum simulation.

</details>


### [92] [Discriminating Lindbladian Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03629)
*Robert Salzmann,Ludovico Lami,Martin B. Plenio,Susana F. Huelga*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了由 Lindblad 生成的两类量子马尔可夫动力学的最优区分问题，刻画了 Stein 指数并揭示时间分辨率作为一种可用资源的作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子假设检验此前多针对量子态和量子信道，而对连续时间量子马尔可夫动力学（Lindblad 动力学）的区分极限，特别是总暴露时间与时间分辨率的作用尚不清楚，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 允许自适应地选择演化时长并在其间施加任意瞬时控制，将最优 type-II 误差指数（Stein 指数）刻画为量子相对熵的最大瞬时增长率，并基于 Lindblad 算子给出其有限的代数判据；借助量子 Zeno 协议构造区分方案。

Result: Stein 指数等于量子相对熵的最大瞬时增长率，其有限性有 Lindblad 代数判据；指数无穷等价于在足够大的有限总时间下对任意正 type-I 容差使 type-II 误差概率为零。部分动力学存在有限协议实现零 type-II 误差，另一些需时间分辨率趋于零才使误差消失。当零假设为单位半群时，固定有限总时间下可使 type-II 误差任意小而 type-I 误差严格为零，此现象在经典情形下不可能。以量子比特上单位半群与广义振幅阻尼的区分为例，展示了三种参数区间。

Conclusion: 时间分辨率是量子动力学区分中的一种资源；无穷 Stein 指数带来有限总时间下零 type-I 误差且任意小 type-II 误差的强结论，并体现出与经典假设检验的本质差异。

Abstract: The fundamental task of quantum hypothesis testing, which consists in discriminating quantum objects, has been extensively studied for quantum states and channels. Here, we investigate the fundamental limits of discriminating an arbitrary pair of quantum Markovian dynamics generated by Lindbladians, focusing on the roles of total exposure time and temporal resolution. We allow adaptive choices of evolution durations and arbitrary instantaneous controls between them. We characterise the optimal type-II error exponent (Stein exponent) as the largest instantaneous increase of quantum relative entropy, and give an algebraic criterion for its finiteness in terms of the Lindbladians. Remarkably, an infinite exponent is equivalent to a vanishing type-II error probability at sufficiently large finite total time, for every positive type-I tolerance. While some dynamics admit a finite protocol with zero type-II error, others require increasingly fine temporal control. For the latter, the error vanishes as the minimum allowed evolution duration, or resolution time, tends to zero at fixed sufficiently large total time, but remains bounded away from zero at every fixed positive resolution time. We achieve this using quantum Zeno protocols, establishing temporal resolution as a resource for discrimination. When the null hypothesis is the identity semigroup, an infinite asymptotic error exponent has a stronger consequence: at a fixed finite total exposure time, the type-II error can be made arbitrarily small while keeping the type-I error exactly zero. This is impossible in the corresponding classical setting. Discriminating the identity semigroup from generalised amplitude damping on a qubit provides examples of all three regimes: a finite asymptotic error exponent, zero type-II error attainable at positive resolution time, and vanishing type-II error requiring the resolution time to tend to zero.

</details>


### [93] [Entanglement of purification for Werner states: canonical purification and nonadditivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03630)
*Zahra Baghali Khanian,Amir-Reza Negari*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 证明冯·诺依曼纠缠纯化（α=1）不可加：在两量子比特Werner态上，典型纯化的纠缠（反射熵）全局最优并等于纠缠纯化，但正则化纠缠纯化严格更低，从而给出不可加性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决纠缠纯化在α=1时的可加性问题（此前仅对0≤α<1的Rényi情形已知不可加）。

Method: 对单重态权重1/64≤f≤1/4的两量子比特Werner态W(f)，证明典型纯化全局最优，其纠缠（反射熵）等于精确纠缠纯化；并比较正则化纠缠纯化与反射熵。方法推广了[1]中用经典两量子比特态证明Rényi不可加的论证。

Result: 典型纯化全局最优，其纠缠（反射熵）给出精确纠缠纯化；当0<f<f_*（f_*≈0.017）时，正则化纠缠纯化严格低于反射熵；在1/64≤f<f_*范围内确立非可加性。

Conclusion: 冯·诺依曼纠缠纯化不可加。不可加性由可分离态见证，底层态无需纠缠。

Abstract: We prove that the von Neumann entanglement of purification is nonadditive. For two-qubit Werner states $W(f)$ with singlet weight $1/64\le f\le1/4$, we show that canonical purification is globally optimal: its entanglement, also known as reflected entropy, gives the exact entanglement of purification. In contrast, the regularized entanglement of purification lies strictly below the reflected entropy for $0<f<f_\ast$, where $f_\ast\approx0.017$. Together, these results establish nonadditivity throughout $1/64\le f<f_\ast$. Our canonical-optimality proof generalizes the proof in [1], which established Rényi nonadditivity for $0\leα<1$ using classical two-qubit states. We thus resolve the additivity question at $α=1$. Remarkably, nonadditivity is again witnessed by separable states and no entanglement in the underlying states is required.

</details>


### [94] [Polynomial-Time Algorithms for Nuclear Tensor Norms and Multipartite Separability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03637)
*Martino Bernasconi,Giulio Malavolta*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究有界Frobenius范数张量的多线性优化问题，提出确定性算法实现常数加性近似，并应用于高阶张量核范数弱成员判定与多体量子可分性测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高阶张量的核范数单位球弱成员判定和多体量子纠缠的可分性测试在高精度下是难点，此前缺乏常数精度下的多项式时间算法，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 将张量优化视为协作式多证明者博弈，结合递归谱压缩过程；并进一步给出该思想的量子实现方案，用于仅能获取量子态副本的情形。

Result: 得到时间 $d^{O(k)}$ 的常数加性近似确定性算法；首次给出高阶张量核范数单位球弱成员判定与Frobenius范数下多体量子可分性问题的常数精度多项式时间算法；在量子副本模型下，用 $\text{poly}(k)$ 个副本与 $\text{poly}(k\log d)$ 时间完成可分性测试。

Conclusion: 多证明者博弈视角与递归谱压缩为张量优化及相关可分性问题提供了统一且高效的算法框架，且在经典与量子两种输入模型下均适用。

Abstract: We study a multilinear optimization problem for tensors with bounded Frobenius norm, where each of the $k$ local factors of dimension $d$ is chosen from a convex set. We give a deterministic algorithm running in time $d^{O(k)}$ for constant additive approximations. As applications, we obtain the first polynomial-time algorithms at constant accuracy for weak membership in the nuclear-norm unit ball of high-order tensors and for multipartite quantum separability in Frobenius norm. Our approach views tensor optimization as a cooperative multiprover game and combines this perspective with a recursive spectral compression procedure. We then show that related ideas admit a quantum implementation when the input state is given through copies, rather than an explicit classical description. In these settings we obtain an algorithm to solve Frobenius-norm separability testing using $\text{poly}(k)$ copies and $\text{poly}(k\log d)$ time.

</details>


### [95] [Clarifications on the Experimental Status of Real Quantum Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03644)
*Timothée Hoffreumon,Mischa P. Woods*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对Renou等人[Nature 600, 625–629 (2021)]声称的双局域网络实验可证伪实数量子理论(RQT)而不证伪量子理论(QT)提出反驳，论证该结论在实验证明层面存在根本缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者此前已证明：若实验要证伪RQT，必然同时证伪QT，这与Renou等人文章的核心主张直接矛盾。本文旨在讨论若干试图恢复该实验结论的自然尝试，并指明其不成立之处。

Method: 采用理论论证方式：首先说明该类结果虽基于非局域性实验，但远未达到Bell型证明的严格程度；其次论证所提实验依赖一个实验上不可检验的前提，即相互独立的制备必然被表示为乘积态；最后讨论超出两方结果范围的相关顾虑。

Result: 论证指出Renou等人的实验方案不具备Bell型证明的完备性，且其核心推理依赖无法通过实验验证的态表示假设(独立制备必为乘积态)，从而使所谓"证伪RQT而不证伪QT"的实验结论无法成立。

Conclusion: Renou等人的实验不足以在不动摇QT的前提下证伪RQT；基于非局域性的此类结论缺乏Bell级别的证明力，且其关键前提在实验上不可检验。

Abstract: In our previous paper on the matter, we proved that real quantum theory (RQT) cannot be experimentally falsified without the experiment falsifying quantum theory (QT) as well. Our result is in direct contradiction to the central claim of an article by Renou et al., published in Nature [600, 625--629 (2021)], reporting a bilocal network experiment that could falsify real quantum theory without falsifying quantum theory.
  The purpose of this note is to address several seemingly natural attempts to recover the experimental conclusion of Renou et al. We first explain how these results, although based on nonlocality experiments, are far from a Bell-like level of proof. Then we explain why the proposed experiment requires an experimentally untestable premise about independent preparations necessarily being represented as product states. Finally, we discuss some concerns that lie outside the scope of both results.

</details>


### [96] [Exponential quantum space advantage in random data streams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03645)
*Adam Bouland,Matthew Ding,Siddhartha Jain*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在随机顺序流式模型下证明了两个无条件的量子空间优势：一是Yamakawa–Zhandry码交集问题具有指数级量子空间优势；二是通过流式版解码量子干涉算法，在低速率区间为最优多项式交集（OPI）问题给出可证明的量子空间优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究在随机顺序流式模型下量子计算相对经典计算的空间优势，扩展现有量子优势理论覆盖更自然的输入到达模式（随机顺序），并为OPI等实际问题建立可证明的量子空间优势。

Method: 采用Göös、Gur、Jain、Li（STOC 2025）的密度恢复分区（density-restoring partitions）技术，结合凸势能（convex potential）方法（类似Raz关于奇偶学习的工作及其推广），证明Yamakawa–Zhandry码交集问题的指数级下界；并构造流式版解码量子干涉算法求解OPI问题。

Result: 在随机顺序流式模型下，Yamakawa–Zhandry码交集问题具有指数级量子空间优势，即使输入仅为随机函数的(x, f(x))对随机顺序到达时也成立。对于次数d、n个评估点的OPI问题，达到1/2 + Ω(√(d/n))约束满足比例，经典需要Ω(n)比特内存，而量子只需O(d log n)量子比特。

Conclusion: 在评估点数远大于次数的"低速率"区间，OPI问题具有可证明的量子空间优势，某些参数设定下空间优势可达指数级。

Abstract: We show two unconditional quantum space advantages in the random-order streaming model. First, we show the Yamakawa--Zhandry Code Intersection problem admits exponential quantum advantage in the streaming model when its inputs are streamed in random order. This means quantum computers exhibit exponential space advantage even when simply receiving $(x,f(x))$ pairs for a uniformly random function $f$ in a uniformly random order. Our lower bound is shown using density-restoring partitions as in the work of Göös, Gur, Jain, and Li (STOC 2025) combined with a convex potential, similar to the work of Raz (J.ACM 2018) on parity learning and its generalization by Garg, Raz, and Tal (STOC 2018).
  Second, we use our framework to show quantum space advantage for the Optimal Polynomial Intersection (OPI) problem in certain regimes via a streaming version of the Decoded Quantum Interferometry algorithm (Nature 2025; arXiv:2510.10967). In particular, we show that for degree $d$ and $n$ evaluation points, attaining $1/2 + Ω(\sqrt{d/n})$ fraction of satisfied OPI constraints via streaming requires $Ω(n)$ classical bits of memory but only $O(d\log n)$ qubits. This yields provable quantum advantage in a "low-rate" regime when the number of evaluation points is much larger than the degree, with a space advantage that can be as large as exponential in certain parameter settings.

</details>


### [97] [Reaching the Limits of Ground-State Metrology with Many-Body Probes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03653)
*Erik L. André,Víctor Izquierdo,Ricard Puig,John Calsamiglia,Martí Perarnau-Llobet*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究利用多体探针（短程或长程相互作用）的基态估计未知参数θ时，达到量子Fisher信息两大极限（静态基态界限F_θ≲N²/Δ²与动力学Heisenberg极限F_θ≲N²τ²）所需的物理条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在基态量子计量中达到终极精度极限的物理前提，明确不同相互作用类型（短程与长程）的量子多体探针饱和静态与动力学极限所需的条件，为设计达到量子精度极限的多体（局域）传感器提供理论基础。

Method: 通过理论分析量子Fisher信息F_θ的静态界限（涉及谱隙Δ）与动力学界限（涉及协议时长τ）；分析短程相互作用体系在二阶量子临界点处达到静态极限所需的临界指数条件ν(d+z)=1，并以XXZ链验证；考察一阶相变中的cat-like基态路径；分析全连接相互作用模型在能隙不为零时的最优性；将动力学界限与基态制备时间关联，采用局域绝热方案获得反能隙（inverse-gap）最优曲线；以横场Ising链、XXZ链、压缩哈密顿量及双模Bose-Hubbard模型等典型系统进行验证。

Result: 短程相互作用体系饱和静态极限需要随N闭合的谱隙，二阶量子临界点对应的普适条件为ν(d+z)=1（可任意逼近，如XXZ链所示）；一阶相变中的cat-like基态提供达到静态最优的另一途径；全连接相互作用模型即使在有能隙区也能达到最优；动力学极限与基态制备时间密切相关，局域绝热协议可实现反能隙最优曲线；在多种典型体系中识别出可逼近两极限的具体多体探针。

Conclusion: 研究结果为设计运行于量子精度极限的（局域）多体基态传感器提供了理论依据。

Abstract: We investigate the physical requirements to reach the ultimate limits of ground-state metrology -- i.e., the estimation of an unknown parameter $θ$ of an $N$-body Hamiltonian via measurements on its ground state -- using many-body probes featuring either short-range or long-range interactions. We characterize the conditions to saturate two fundamental limits of the quantum Fisher information $F_θ$: the static ground-state bound $F_θ\lesssim N^2/Δ^2$, where $Δ$ is the spectral gap, and the dynamical Heisenberg limit $F_θ\lesssim N^2 τ^2$, where $τ$ is the total protocol duration. To saturate the static limit, we argue that short-range interacting systems require a spectral gap that closes with $N$. At second-order quantum critical points this leads to a universal condition on the critical exponents, $ν(d+z)=1$, which can be approached arbitrarily closely, as we show for the XXZ chain; cat-like ground states at first-order transitions provide an alternative route to static optimality. In contrast, we find that all-to-all interacting models can achieve optimality even in gapped regimes. Furthermore, reaching the dynamical bound is tightly related to the ground-state preparation time; we demonstrate that its optimal, inverse-gap profile can be obtained via local adiabatic protocols. By analyzing paradigmatic systems -- the transverse-field Ising chain, the XXZ chain, a squeezing Hamiltonian, and the two-mode Bose-Hubbard model -- we identify specific many-body probes capable of approaching both limits. Overall, our results provide a theoretical basis for the design of (local) many-body ground-state sensors operating at the ultimate limits of quantum precision.

</details>


### [98] [A Basis-Aware Approach to Quantum Sampling of the Fermi-Hubbard Ladder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03654)
*Adam Leicester,Zixu Wang,Jack Mandell,Yangyang Xu,Osama Muhammad Raisuddin,Haimeng Zhang,Jian Shi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究了sample-based Krylov量子对角化(基于量子采样的Krylov对角化)中，采样本征基的紧致性对经典对角化效率的影响，通过在half-filled Fermi-Hubbard ladder上使用物理启发的单body轨道旋转，揭示了最优表示随相互作用强度与hopping各向异性的系统性变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于采样(Sample-based)的量子对角化可将经典多体计算的成本降至仅对通过量子采样识别出的组态做对角化，但其效率取决于目标态在所采样基下的紧致程度。论文旨在考察这种依赖关系，探索何种表示能使目标态更可压缩，从而提升整体算法效率。

Method: 以半填充Fermi-Hubbard ladder为模型体系，采用物理上motivated的单body(单粒子)轨道旋转，构造位置、动量、成键/反成键(bonding/antibonding)以及leg-mode等多种表示；结合statevector的sample-based Krylov quantum diagonalization(SKQD)计算以及最多含120 qubits的硬件计算，比较不同表示下的可压缩性与采样效率。

Result: 最可压缩的基随相互作用强度和hopping各向异性系统性变化：弱相互作用区动量采样更优，而随主导能量尺度改变，几何或相互作用自适应的表示更具优势；在中等相互作用且各向同性hopping附近，即便最有利的表示仍难以压缩。Statevector SKQD计算表明基的选择强烈影响采样效率；硬件计算保留了这一基依赖性的定性特征，同时随体系增大出现粒子数泄漏(particle-number leakage)增加和重构敏感性上升。

Conclusion: 结果表明可扩展的基于采样的对角化需要采样本征表示、量子线路与经典重构流程三者的协同设计(co-design)。

Abstract: Sample-based quantum diagonalization can reduce the classical cost of many-body calculations by restricting diagonalization to configurations identified through quantum sampling, but its efficiency depends on how compactly the target state is represented in the sampled basis. We investigate this dependence for sample-based Krylov quantum diagonalization of the half-filled Fermi-Hubbard ladder using physically motivated one-body orbital rotations. Across position, momentum, bonding/antibonding, and leg-mode representations, the most compressible basis varies systematically with interaction strength and hopping anisotropy, with momentum sampling favored in the weakly interacting regime and geometry- or interaction-adapted representations becoming advantageous as the dominant energy scale changes. Near intermediate interaction and isotropic hopping, however, even the most favorable representation remains poorly compressible. Statevector SKQD calculations further show that basis choice strongly affects sampling efficiency. Hardware calculations on systems containing up to 120 qubits retain qualitative signatures of this basis dependence while revealing increasing particle-number leakage and reconstruction sensitivity with system size. These results show that scalable sample-based diagonalization requires co-design of the sampled representation, quantum circuit, and classical reconstruction procedure.

</details>


### [99] [Breaking the cubic barrier for the inverse-free Solovay-Kitaev algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03655)
*Adam Bouland,Victor Shyaka,Chenyi Zhang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究无逆门集合的高效量子编译问题，针对Bouland与Giurgicǎ-Tiron算法指数偏高（qubit超过8.62）的不足，提出两种改进算法：Pauli门加无理旋转的特殊情形指数≤约2.138，一般无逆qubit情形指数≤2.988，首次给出超越信息论下界（3）的编译指数实例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Solovay-Kitaev定理给出polylog(1/ε)时间的编译算法，但其原始工作及多数后续结果依赖逆封闭门集合。Bouland与Giurgicǎ-Tiron虽将结果推广到无逆门集合，然而其编译指数很高（qubit情形超过8.62），而Oszmaniec、Sawicki与Horodecki的信息论结果表明任意维度下无逆指数可达3。因此自然的问题是：能否降低Bouland-Giurgicǎ-Tiron算法的指数？是否存在指数低于3、从而超越现有信息论论证的情形？

Method: 作者首先处理一个特殊情形——Pauli门辅以qubit的无理旋转，融合Kuperberg以及Sardharwalla、Cubitt、Harrow、Linden先前算法的思想，构造高效的“Pauli golf”编译程序，得到指数至多约2.138。随后将上述思想推广到qubit的一般无逆情形，采用一种新颖的酉修正近似“瞄准”（aiming）方法来提升编译效率，给出指数至多2.988的编译算法。

Result: 特殊情形（Pauli门加无理旋转）的编译指数至多约2.138；qubit一般无逆情形的编译指数至多2.988。两者均低于信息论所知的指数3，构成两个超越信息论论证的量子编译算法实例。

Conclusion: 本文证明无逆编译问题存在显著效率提升空间：其算法指数不仅大幅优于Bouland-Giurgicǎ-Tiron的结果，还在两个具体情形中突破信息论给出的指数3下界，表明无逆编译在效率上仍有大量可挖掘的改进余地。

Abstract: The Solovay-Kitaev algorithm states that it is possible to efficiently compile unitaries to accuracy $ε$ in $\text{polylog}(1/ε)$ time using any universal gate set. While Solovay and Kitaev's initial work addressed inverse-closed gate sets, recently Bouland and Giurgicǎ-Tiron showed it is possible to efficiently compile arbitrary gate sets without inverses. However, the exponent of their compilation algorithm is high. For example the inverse-free exponent for a qubit is over $8.62$, while information theoretically it is known that an inverse-free exponent of $3$ is possible in any dimension by a result of Oszmaniec, Sawicki, and Horodecki. This leads to a natural question: can one improve the exponent in Bouland and Giurgicǎ-Tiron's algorithm? And are there any cases where the inverse-free exponent is below 3, thus surpassing existing information-theoretic arguments?
  To answer this question, we first show that a special case of the inverse-free compiling problem -- namely Pauli gates supplemented by an irrational rotation of a qubit - admits a highly efficient compilation algorithm with an exponent at most $\approx 2.138$.
  Our algorithm fuses ideas from prior algorithms of Kuperberg and Sardharwalla, Cubitt, Harrow and Linden to construct an efficient ``Pauli golf'' compilation routine. We then extend these ideas to the general inverse-free case for a qubit, where we give a compiling algorithm with exponent at most $2.988$. This result uses a novel form of approximate ``aiming'' of unitary corrections to improve the compilation efficiency. Our results give two examples of quantum compiling algorithms surpassing information-theoretic arguments, and demonstrate that there are many efficiency gains possible for inverse-free compilation.

</details>


### [100] [Super-Exponential Advantage of Squeezed Light in Phase Estimation under Discrete Phase Randomisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03657)
*Lorcan O. Conlon,V Vijendran,Aritra Das,Yu-Xin Wang,Erfan Abbasgholinejad,Anthony J. Brady,Jacob Bringewatt,Sean R. Muleady,Syed M. Assad,Alexey V. Gorshkov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究相位估计任务中量子资源带来的优势上限。通过引入均匀随机未知整数 k 的相位平移通道，证明在每通道平均光子数约束下，压缩真空态与相干态的量子费舍尔信息之间存在超指数级分离，并给出非渐近估计结果及噪声、损耗情形下的鲁棒性分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子计量学中普遍认为，在合理先验信息假设下，量子资源带来的灵敏度优势上限为二次（Heisenberg 极限）。作者希望检验这一信念是否绝对成立，考察在特定通道与资源约束下能否突破二次优势，从而重新界定量子资源相比经典相干态的极限能力。

Method: 构造一个相位平移通道：相位移动 2πk/M，其中 k 为均匀随机未知整数，并叠加待估计的小相位。在每通道平均光子数约束下，比较压缩真空态与相干态（及经相位协变通道从相干态得到的任意态）所能达到的量子费舍尔信息 QFI，并进一步进行非渐近估计分析。随后引入噪声、固定光学损耗与热噪声，比较压缩真空态结合外差探测与指定经典态族协议的通道使用数。

Result: 在每通道平均光子数为 1 时，压缩真空态与相干态 QFI 之比按 Ω[(M/√2e)^M] 增长；平均光子数为 M/log(M) 时，压缩真空 QFI 为 Ω(M^{3/2}/√log M)，而相干态至多为 exp[−M log log M + O(M)]。非渐近结果：平均光子数 M/log M 时，压缩真空协议以 O(√(M log M)) 次通道使用达到 O(1/M) 精度，而所有相干态协议至少需 exp[M log log M − O(M)] 次通道使用。噪声下 QFI 之比仍可超指数增长。固定损耗与热噪声下，取 n̄ = M²/(log M)^{3/2}，压缩真空加外差探测以亚多项式次通道使用达到 O(1/M) 精度，而指定经典族协议需超多项式次通道使用。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，在特定相位估计通道与光子数约束下，压缩真空态相对相干态可获得远超二次（超指数级）的量子优势，且该优势在非渐近估计、噪声、光学损耗与热噪声情形下依然稳健。这突破了量子计量中“二次优势为上限”的传统认知，并凸显压缩真空态作为量子资源的重要性。

Abstract: A paradigmatic task in quantum metrology is that of phase estimation. For this task it is commonly believed, under reasonable assumptions about prior information, that a quadratic advantage in sensitivity is the maximum possible advantage offered by the use of quantum resources. In this work, we consider a channel that implements a phase shift by an angle $2πk/M$, where $k$ is a uniformly random unknown integer, in addition to a small unknown phase shift of interest. We show that, subject to a constraint on the mean photon number per channel use, there is a super-exponential separation between the quantum Fisher information (QFI) attainable using a squeezed vacuum state and that attainable using coherent states. This advantage holds over any state obtained from a coherent state via phase covariant channels. Specifically, when the mean photon number per channel use is 1, the ratio of their QFI grows as $Ω[(M/\sqrt{2}e)^M]$. For a mean photon number per channel use of $M/\log(M)$, the squeezed vacuum QFI is $Ω(M^{3/2}/\sqrt{\log(M)})$, whereas for a coherent state it is at most $\exp[-M\log\log(M)+O(M)]$. We complement this QFI analysis with non-asymptotic estimation results. For a mean photon number per channel use of $M/\log M$, an explicit squeezed-vacuum protocol estimates the phase to accuracy $O(1/M)$ using $O(\sqrt{M\log M})$ channel uses, whereas every coherent state protocol requires at least $\exp[M\log\log M-O(M)]$ channel uses. We consider the effects of noise and show that the ratio of their QFIs can still grow super-exponentially in $M$. Finally, under fixed optical loss and thermal noise, choosing $\bar n=M^2/(\log M)^{3/2}$ allows squeezed vacuum with heterodyne detection to achieve accuracy $O(1/M)$ using sub-polynomially many channel uses, whereas every protocol in the specified classical family requires super-polynomially many.

</details>


### [101] [A note on tomography of states with low stabilizer rank](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03658)
*Arkopal Dutt*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究稳定子秩为 k 的 n 量子比特量子态层析问题，证明稳定子扩展度满足 ξ(|ψ⟩)≤2^{O(k)}，并据此给出多项式时间层析协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稳定子分解为量子态提供了有效的经典描述，但稳定子秩为 k 的态空间随 k 增长极快。已有层析方法受限于稳定子扩展度上界 k^{O(k)}，只能处理很小的 k，因此需要更紧的扩展度界以扩展可高效层析的态范围。

Method: 首先改进稳定子扩展度 ξ(|ψ⟩) 的上界：对任意可写成 k 个稳定子态线性组合的态，证明其最小 ℓ1 系数范数满足 ξ(|ψ⟩)≤2^{O(k)}（此前为 k^{O(k)}）。随后利用针对有界稳定子扩展度态的层析协议，结合该新界构造总体协议。

Result: 给出了对稳定子秩为 k 的 n 量子比特态进行迹距离 ε 精度层析的协议，运行时间为 poly(n,2^k,1/ε)，因此可在 k=O(log n) 时高效处理，复杂度为 poly(n,1/ε)。

Conclusion: 通过将稳定子扩展度上界从 k^{O(k)} 改进为 2^{O(k)}，显著扩大了可高效层析的稳定子秩范围，使 k=O(log n) 的量子态可在多项式时间内完成 ε 精度层析。

Abstract: In this note, we consider tomography of $n$-qubit quantum states $|ψ\rangle$ that are promised to have stabilizer rank $k$ i.e., admit a stabilizer decomposition of $|ψ\rangle = \sum_{i=1}^k c_i |φ_i\rangle$ where each $|φ_i\rangle$ is a stabilizer state. We give a tomography protocol for such states up to trace distance $\varepsilon > 0$ running in $\textsf{poly}(n,2^k,1/\varepsilon)$ time and thus can handle $k=O(\log n)$ efficiently. This follows from first showing that the stabilizer extent $ξ(|ψ\rangle)$, which is the minimal $\ell_1$ norm over the coefficients $\{c_i\}$ of any stabilizer decomposition of $|ψ\rangle$, can be bounded as $ξ(|ψ\rangle) \leq 2^{O(k)}$ and improving upon the previous best bound of $k^{O(k)}$. The protocol is then obtained by utilizing a tomography protocol for states with bounded stabilizer extent.

</details>


### [102] [Quantum Codeword Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03659)
*John Blue,Zachary E. Chin,Vlad S. Oros,Isaac L. Chuang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究在总信号较弱(Qθ→0)时,利用持续量子记忆的传感器接收机在识别未知经典码字任务中,性能严格优于仅具经典记忆的接收机,并可实现传输错误的精确纠正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在量子传感器通过弱相位交互接收经典码字的场景下,量子记忆相比经典记忆能否带来本质性的接收性能优势,以及这种优势在存在比特翻转时的表现。

Method: 将传感器寄存器耦合到与之同尺寸的持续量子记忆,允许辅助比特、任意控制幺正操作及纠缠;针对偶数k、n=2^k−1的[n,k]二进制单纯形码,使用Q=Θ((t+1)log n)与θ=2π/(k√(n+1))进行接收。

Result: 对t≤(k−2)/4的传输错误实现精确纠错;当t=o(√k)时,任何同等条件但仅有经典记忆的接收机平均成功概率趋于零。在独立比特翻转(固定p<1/16)下,使用Q=O(log n log log n)总比特即可使解码错误趋于零,而仅有经典记忆的接收机平均成功概率消失。

Conclusion: 持续量子记忆能在弱信号下赋予接收机超越经典记忆的根本优势,既体现在确定性纠错的码率-资源标度上,也体现在随机噪声模型下的渐近可靠性上。

Abstract: Consider the task of identifying an unknown classical codeword delivered to a register of quantum sensor qubits through a sequence of weak phase interactions that each encode one bit. Suppose that each received bit controls an identical $Z$-rotation of angle $θ$ applied to every sensor qubit; receivers may also use auxiliary qubits within a total qubit budget $Q$, with arbitrary control unitaries and entanglement among all qubits allowed during each reception step. When the total collected signal is weak ($Qθ\rightarrow 0$), we establish that a sensor coupled to a persistent quantum memory can identify the original codeword more reliably than any receiver with the same $Q$ and $θ$ but only classical memory between received bits, even when bit flips occur during transmission. In particular, for a family of $[n,k]$ binary simplex codes with even $k$ and $n=2^k-1$, we achieve exact correction of up to $t\leq(k-2)/4$ transmission errors by coupling the sensor register to a persistent quantum memory of the same size, using $Q = Θ((t+1)\log n)$ and $θ=\frac{2π}{k\sqrt{n+1}}$; for $t=o(\sqrt k)$, every comparable receiver with only classical memory instead has average success probability (over uniformly chosen codewords) tending to zero. Furthermore, under independent bit flips of any fixed probability $p<1/16$, our receiver with persistent quantum memory achieves decoding error tending to zero as $n\to\infty$ using $Q=O(\log n\log\log n)$ total qubits and the same $θ$. In contrast, every otherwise equivalent receiver with only classical memory has vanishing average success probability under the same stochastic error model.

</details>


### [103] [Lie Algebraic Uncertainty Relations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03661)
*Xiaoli Hu,Naihuan Jing,Jian Zhang,Yunlong Xiao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出基于协方差的量子不确定性表述，为厄米与非厄米可观测量给出无穷族不确定性关系；当算子构成李代数基时，可由代数及其表示直接得到最优界的闭式解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子不确定性关系体现可观测量的根本不相容性，但目前仅有少数情形具有解析的最优态无关界，缺乏统一、可计算的框架。

Method: 引入基于协方差的量子不确定性表述，构造适用于厄米可观测量和非厄米算子的无穷族关系；对构成李代数基的算子，利用代数结构及其表示将量子态优化替换为直接的代数计算，并分析权重空间内不确定性的取值行为。

Result: 对构成李代数基的算子，可从代数与表示获得最优界的闭式表达；在每个权重空间内不确定性取固定值，使不等式转化为守恒律。

Conclusion: 交换关系及其在量子态上的实现共同决定不相容算子的最小量子不确定性；该框架将不确定性与对称性、表示论相联系，为通过底层李代数揭示量子信息处理的內禀极限提供了途径。

Abstract: Uncertainty relations express the fundamental incompatibility of quantum observables, yet optimal state-independent bounds are known analytically in only a few cases. Here we introduce a covariance-based formulation of quantum uncertainty that yields an infinite family of relations for both Hermitian observables and non-Hermitian operators. For operators forming a basis of a Lie algebra, we derive the optimal bound in closed form from the algebra and its representation, replacing optimization over quantum states with a direct algebraic calculation. Within each weight space, uncertainty takes a fixed value, turning the inequality into a conservation law. These results show how commutation relations and their realization on a quantum state jointly determine the minimum quantum uncertainty associated with incompatible operators. By connecting uncertainty to symmetry and representation theory, the framework offers a route to uncovering intrinsic limits on quantum information processing through its underlying Lie algebra.

</details>


### [104] [BARC codes: general polynomial framework for coherent-state superposition codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03663)
*Praneel Gore,Davit Aghamalyan,Varun Narasimhachar,Andrew Tanggara*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出一种构造量子纠错码的代数框架——玻色代数受限星座（BARC）码，以复多项式系统解集的对称性约束相干态星座，并给出最小几何分离上界、单模椭球与六边形显式构造，及在纯损耗噪声下的基准测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 玻色量子纠错码需要兼顾光子增益/损耗错误的鲁棒性与可解析构造。现有星座类码（如球形码、cubature码）缺乏与代数对称性及噪声结构相匹配的系统性设计框架，故需引入代数约束来逼近Knill–Laflamme条件。

Method: 构建代数框架：码态为受多元复多项式系统解集对称性约束的相干态星座的有限叠加；将约束与光子增益和光子损耗错误关联，对等权星座点推导最小几何分离的上界；单模情形下利用正交群对称性刻画二次多项式解集，得到显式椭球与六边形BARC码。

Result: 推导出等权星座点最小几何分离的上界，用以表征码的抗噪能力；在单模情形下获得显式的椭球与六边形BARC码族；并将代表性实例与球形码及cubature码在纯损耗噪声、最优恢复下的纠缠保真度进行基准对比。

Conclusion: BARC码提供了一种基于代数对称性的量子纠错码构造途径，可针对增益/损耗误差近似满足Knill–Laflamme条件，并在单模二次多项式情形给出可实现的具体码族，为玻色编码设计与评估提供新工具。

Abstract: We introduce an algebraic framework for constructing a new class of quantum error-correcting codes, bosonic algebraically-restricted constellation (BARC) codes. The code states are finite superpositions of coherent-state constellations constrained by symmetries of solution sets to multivariate complex polynomial systems. The constraints are associated with photon-gain and photon-loss errors, thereby imposing approximate Knill--Laflamme conditions for such errors. For equally-weighted constellation points, we derive upper bounds on the minimum geometric separation between points, indicating how noise-resilient the code is. In the single-mode case, we characterize degree-two polynomial solution sets using an orthogonal group symmetry to obtain explicit ellipsoidal and hexagonal BARC codes. We benchmark representative instances of these families against spherical and cubature codes using the entanglement fidelity with optimal recovery under pure-loss noise.

</details>


### [105] [Optimal kernel functions for linear combination of Hamiltonian simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03669)
*Harriet Apel,Bjorn K. Berntson*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将线性组合哈密顿量模拟(LCHS)的核函数类推广到满足复解析条件的 admissible 类，证明其近似定理，在标准时间无关块编码模型下构造唯一最优核函数，得到查询代价的最小渐近展开 2e/π(log(1/ε)−loglog(1/ε)+o(1))，比 Low–Somma 的数值结果改善约2.1倍，并构造谱收敛求积以保证离散化后该优势保持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LCHS 为线性非幺正动力学量子模拟提供渐近最优框架，其精度由核函数的积分表示决定。此前工作中最优核函数仅在特定家族内通过数值最小化获得，缺乏对更广核函数类的理论刻画与可证明最优性。作者希望扩大 admissible 核函数类、严格建立近似定理，并给出查询代价的可证明最优渐近系数。

Method: 基于 Hardy 空间理论与凸分析，定义由自然复解析条件刻画的广义 admissible 核函数类，证明 LCHS 近似定理；在时间无关块编码访问模型下，针对每个目标误差 ε 显式构造该类中使查询代价泛函唯一的极小元，推导其最小值的渐近展开；构造谱收敛求积，给出算符误差与 LCU 次归一化的显式界，证明离散化为有限个幺正线性组合后渐近改进仍保持。

Result: 得到查询代价泛函最小值的渐近展开 2e/π(log(1/ε)−loglog(1/ε)+o(1))（ε↓0），其可证明最优主系数 2e/π 比 Low–Somma 经数值最小化得到的经验系数改善约 2.1 倍；所构造的离散求积具有显式误差与次归一化界，保证了实现层面的最优性。

Conclusion: 本文将 LCHS 的核函数理论推广并刻画了使查询代价最优的唯一核函数，给出了可证明最优的渐近主系数并对离散化后实现进行了严格分析。所用 Hardy 空间与凸分析方法为核函数类最优性与 LCU 实现提供了严格工具，显著改进了已有经验结果。

Abstract: Linear combination of Hamiltonian simulation (LCHS) provides an asymptotically optimal framework for the quantum simulation of linear non-unitary dynamics. The LCHS approach approximately implements the Peano--Baker propagator through an integral representation determined by a kernel function. We establish an LCHS approximation theorem for a broadened class of admissible kernel functions, defined by natural complex-analytic conditions. In the standard time-independent block-encoding access model, we explicitly construct, for each target error $\varepsilon$, the unique kernel function in this class minimizing the query-cost functional and show that the minimum of this functional has the asymptotic expansion $\tfrac{2\mathrm{e}}π\big(\log\frac{1}{\varepsilon}-\log\log\frac{1}{\varepsilon}+o(1)\big)$ as ${\varepsilon\downarrow0}$. The provably optimal leading coefficient $2\mathrm{e}/π$ improves by a factor of approximately 2.1 on the coefficient obtained empirically by Low and Somma through numerical minimization over their family of optimally scaling kernel functions. We construct a spectrally convergent quadrature with explicit bounds on operator error and linear combination of unitaries (LCU) subnormalization, proving that this asymptotic improvement persists after discretization into a finite linear combination of unitaries suitable for implementation. Our results are based on Hardy space theory and convex analysis.

</details>


### [106] [Classical Algorithms for Bipartite Quantum Max-Cut on Dense Expanders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03670)
*Stuart Wayland,Zackary Jorquera,Alexandra Kolla*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对自旋1/2反铁磁Heisenberg模型（等价于Quantum Max-Cut）在稠密平衡二部扩展图上的基态问题，给出了多项式时间的经典随机算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Quantum Max-Cut是一个被广泛研究的局部哈密顿量问题，其二部情形的经典复杂度仍然悬而未决。作者希望在这一开放问题上取得进展，针对稠密平衡二部扩展图这一类实例给出高效经典算法，从而厘清该问题在特定图类上的可计算性边界。

Method: 算法在完全二部图的完美匹配集合上构造马尔可夫链，每个匹配代表一组单态（singlet）的乘积态。该链收敛到基态，作者利用图的扩展性证明多项式步数与采样数即可充分逼近。

Result: 算法能在关于n和1/ε多项式的时间内，将基态能量及任意基态边的关联估计到加性误差ε。该结论覆盖以高概率出现的G(n,n,p)（对任意固定p>0），即稠密平衡二部扩展图这一大类实例。

Conclusion: 结果表明，在稠密平衡二部扩展图上，Quantum Max-Cut（自旋1/2反铁磁Heisenberg模型）存在多项式时间的经典随机算法，为二部情形复杂度的理解提供了正面证据。

Abstract: Quantum Max-Cut is a well-studied local Hamiltonian problem for which the classical complexity of the bipartite case remains open. We give a polynomial-time classical randomized algorithm for the spin-$\tfrac12$ antiferromagnetic Heisenberg model, equivalently Quantum Max-Cut, on dense balanced bipartite expanders. This class includes $G(n,n,p)$ with high probability for every fixed $p>0$. The algorithm estimates the ground energy and any ground state edge correlation to additive error $\varepsilon$ in time polynomial in $n$ and $1/\varepsilon$. We construct a Markov chain on perfect matchings of the complete bipartite graph, each matching representing a product of singlets, that converges to the ground state, and use the expansion of the graph to show that polynomially many steps and samples of this chain suffice.

</details>


### [107] [Quantum simulation of field-tunable spin spectroscopy of the quantum magnet Cs2CoCl4 on a trapped-ion quantum computer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03671)
*Elias Kokkas,Nora Bauer,Justin Provazza,Mark E. Nowakowski,Kathleen Hamilton,Gilles Buchs,Andrew Sornborger,Travis S. Humble,Ananth Kaushik,Martin Roetteler*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 利用IonQ 36比特处理器，通过含420个两比特门的压缩量子线路模拟36格点Heisenberg XXZ链的磁激发谱与自旋动力学，恢复的动力学结构因子与DMRG吻合良好（Pearson相关和结构相似度>0.9，NMSE<0.15）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子磁体的集体激发谱是检验量子计算机研究强关联物质能力的直接手段，但硬件噪声限制了可用线路深度，因此需探究线路压缩后仍能保留多少磁性信息。

Method: 结合无辅助比特的local kick协议与经典张量网络压缩，将基态制备和时间演化编码进仅含420个两比特门的量子线路；在IonQ Forte Enterprise（36比特）上模拟横场下描述量子磁体Cs2CoCl4的36格点Heisenberg XXZ链；采用误差缓解方法。

Result: 重建的动力学结构因子呈现从宽的双自旋子连续谱向近临界场附近具有磁振子特征的色散分支的演化；观测到场驱动的最近邻自旋关联符号反转。误差缓解后总谱与DMRG计算高度一致（Pearson相关和结构相似度>0.9，归一化均方误差<0.15）；并与非弹性中子散射实验比较以关联材料磁响应。

Conclusion: 线路压缩与误差缓解使含噪量子处理器能够同时恢复集体激发和局域磁关联，为量子谱学提供定量基准，并为将该方法推广到更复杂磁性系统奠定基础。

Abstract: The collective excitation spectra of quantum magnets provide a direct test of quantum computers as tools for studying strongly correlated matter. Hardware noise limits the circuit depths available for these calculations, leaving the question of how much magnetic information can survive circuit compression. Using IonQ's 36 qubit Forte Enterprise processor, we simulate the magnetic excitation spectra and spin dynamics of a 36 site Heisenberg XXZ chain describing the quantum magnet Cs2CoCl4 in a transverse field using quantum circuits containing just 420 two qubit gates. The workflow combines an ancilla free local kick protocol with classical tensor network compression, encoding both ground state preparation and time evolution. The reconstructed dynamical structure factors capture the evolution from a broad two spinon continuum toward a dispersive branch with magnon character near the expected critical field. We observe an interesting field driven sign reversal of the nearest neighbor spin correlations which shows a complementary signature of this magnetic evolution. Our error mitigation methods bring the reconstructed total spectra into close agreement with density matrix renormalization group calculations, with Pearson correlation and structural similarity above 0.9 and normalized mean squared error below 0.15. We also perform comparisons with experimental inelastic neutron scattering measurements to connect the recovered excitations to the material's magnetic response. Our results demonstrate that circuit compression and error mitigation allow noisy quantum processors to recover both collective excitations and local magnetic correlations providing a quantitative benchmark for quantum spectroscopy and a basis for extending these methods to more complex magnetic systems.

</details>


### [108] [Ordering-Aware Theory of Trotter Error](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03672)
*Zhenning Liu,Zhi-Yuan Wei,Michael Foss-Feig,Alexey V. Gorshkov,Andrew M. Childs*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文系统研究哈密顿量项排序对乘积公式模拟精度的影响，提出可高效计算且对排序敏感的Trotter误差上界，并据此优化排序以提升模拟精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 乘积公式是模拟量子多体动力学的基础工具，但其应用中的一个基本自由度——哈密顿量项的排序——此前基本未被探索。作者希望量化排序对模拟精度的影响，并利用排序来设计更精确的乘积公式模拟。

Method: 针对几何局域哈密顿量，作者发展了可高效计算、对排序敏感的有限步长Trotter误差上界，适用于任意高阶乘积公式；利用误差界和估计的局域结构优化排序，将长链问题归约为加权de Bruijn图上的最小平均权重环问题。

Result: 在平移不变一维链的小系统测试中，该上界接近精确的二阶、四阶和六阶Trotter误差，在大规模系统上比此前的高阶解析界改进了数个数量级。对随机平移不变链，顺序（阶梯状）排序持续优于标准砖墙电路；当电路深度受限时，优化器倾向于波状并行-顺序排序，并保留了对砖墙电路的大部分精度优势。

Conclusion: 排序是理解和改进乘积公式模拟的一个有用自由度；通过对排序的敏感误差界与优化，可以显著提升乘积公式模拟的精度。

Abstract: Product formulas are a basic tool for simulating quantum many-body dynamics, but a fundamental degree of freedom in their application---the ordering of Hamiltonian terms---has remained largely unexplored. Here we systematically study such ordering effects, quantifying their impact on simulation accuracy and exploiting them to design more accurate product-formula simulations. We develop an efficiently computable, ordering-sensitive bound on the finite-step-size Trotter error for geometrically local Hamiltonians for arbitrarily high-order product formulas. For translation-invariant one-dimensional chains, our bounds are close to exact second-, fourth-, and sixth-order Trotter errors in small-system tests, while on large-scale systems they improve on previous higher-order analytical bounds by orders of magnitude. We further use the local structure of the error bounds and estimates to optimize ordering efficiently, reducing the long-chain problem to a minimum-mean-weight-cycle problem on a weighted de Bruijn graph. For random translation-invariant chains, sequential (staircase-like) orderings consistently outperform standard brickwall circuits; when circuit depth is restricted, the optimizer instead favors wave-like parallel-sequential orderings, which preserve much of the accuracy advantage over brickwall circuits. These results establish ordering as a useful degree of freedom for both understanding and improving product-formula simulation.

</details>


### [109] [Quantum Simulation on Riemannian Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03673)
*Dylan Herman,Jacob Watkins,Guneykan Ozgul,Jiayu Shen,Brandon Augustino,Junhyung Lyle Kim,Shouvanik Chakrabarti*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究黎曼流形上薛定谔方程的量子模拟算法：一是基于Laplace–Beltrami算子本征基的全局谱方法，对环面、球面、单纯形（Wright–Fisher度量）、正卦限与ℓp球等给出显式高效算法；二是基于多图卡局部谱方法的一致性量子模拟，适用于任意紧流形，并分析多项式谱截断与离散化条件。应用涵盖几何凸优化的量子哈密顿下降与实空间绝热算法推广，以及特定空间离散场论（含非线性sigma模型变体）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黎曼流形上的薛定谔方程在优化与物理仿真中有重要应用，但其动力学由与度规相关的Laplace–Beltrami算子刻画，难以直接进行量子模拟。现有研究缺乏针对具体流形的高效量子算法及其在优化、场论中的应用分析，因此需要发展全局与局部谱方法并建立严格的截断与离散化理论。

Method: 第一类算法采用全局谱方法：构造到Laplace–Beltrami算子本征基的高效变换；针对环面与球面（标准度量）、单纯形（Wright–Fisher度量）、截断正卦限及其可逆仿射像（对数障碍Hessian度量）、ℓp球（Duffy映射诱导度量）给出显式量子模拟算法。第二类算法基于多个图卡上的局部谱方法的一致性量子模拟，原则上适用于任意紧流形；先在连续情形推导多项式谱截断充分条件，再对常维数流形张量积进行多项式谱截断的离散化分析。应用方面包括：将量子哈密顿下降推广并给出球面与单纯形上几何凸函数的收敛性分析与显式算法、黎曼版实空间绝热算法，以及对含非线性sigma模型变体的空间离散场论模拟。

Result: 得到环面、球面、单纯形、正卦限仿射像与ℓp球的显式高效量子模拟算法；给出局部谱方法一致性模拟的连续条件与多项式截断下张量积流形的离散化分析；证明量子哈密顿下降在几何凸函数上的推广与收敛性并给出球面、单纯形显式算法；提出黎曼实空间绝热算法；证明可模拟某些空间离散场论，包括非线性sigma模型变体。

Conclusion: 该工作为黎曼流形上的量子薛定谔模拟提供了全局谱与多图卡局部谱两类系统框架，既可覆盖典型流形并保持高效，又具备对一般紧流形的适用性与截断/离散化保证，并打通了到黎曼优化与场论物理仿真的应用路径。

Abstract: We investigate algorithms for the quantum simulation of the Schrödinger equation on a Riemannian manifold, where the kinetic operator is defined by the Laplace--Beltrami operator corresponding to the metric. Our first algorithms are based on a global spectral method based on the identification of an efficient transform to the eigenbasis of the Laplace--Beltrami operator. We use this method to provide explicit, efficient, quantum simulation algorithms for the Riemannian Schrödinger equation on tori and spheres with their standard metrics, simplices with the Wright--Fisher metric, truncated positive orthants and their invertible affine images with the log-barrier Hessian metric, and $\ell_p$ balls with a metric induced by the Duffy map. Our second algorithm is based on a coherent simulation of local spectral methods on multiple charts, and is in principle applicable to any compact manifold. We first analyze this algorithm in the continuum and derive conditions under which a polynomial spectral cutoff suffices. We also provide a discretization analysis of a polynomial spectral cutoff for tensor-products of constant-dimensional manifolds. Finally, we consider applications of these methods to optimization and physical simulation. For optimization, we provide results including a generalization and convergence analysis of Quantum Hamiltonian Descent for geodesically convex functions that leads to explicit algorithms on the sphere and simplex, and a Riemannian generalization of the Real-Space Adiabatic Algorithm. For physical simulation, we show that our algorithms can simulate certain spatially discretized field theories, including a variant of the nonlinear sigma model.

</details>


### [110] [Efficient capacity-achieving entanglement generation with application to pure-loss Bosonic channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03674)
*Avijit Mandal,Christophe Piveteau,Henry D. Pfister,Joseph M. Renes*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文构建了一种高效纠缠生成方案，对一大类信道达到相干信息，对可退化信道达到量子容量；该方案基于两类CQ信道的极化码，首次给出可达能量约束下纯损耗玻色信道量子容量的显式高效可译协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子通信在噪声信道上的速率极限已有明确理论，但能达到这些极限的高效编码方案极少，与经典通信中极化码和LDPC码可高效达到任意噪声信道经典容量形成鲜明对比。因此本文旨在构造能高效逼近量子容量极限的编码方案。

Method: 构造高效纠缠生成方案，利用两类经典输入-量子输出（CQ）信道的极化码：输出可交换的信道（可用经典极化译码算法）与输出为纯态预示混合的信道（用带量子消息的置信传播BPQM算法译码）。

Result: 该方案对一大类信道达到相干信息；对其中可退化信道（包括纯损耗玻色信道的截断版本）达到量子容量；并首次给出对纯损耗玻色信道在能量约束下达到量子容量的显式且高效可译协议。

Conclusion: 该工作将极化码思想拓展至量子信道编码，实现了对广泛信道类的高效纠缠生成与量子容量达成，填补了量子通信中缺乏高效可达容量编码方案的空白。

Abstract: While the ultimate limits on the rate of quantum communication over noisy channels are well-established, very few efficient coding schemes achieving these limits are known. This contrasts sharply with transmitting classical data over classical channels, where polar codes and low-density parity-check codes both efficiently achieve the classical capacity of any noisy channel. In this work we construct an efficient entanglement generation scheme which achieves the coherent information for a broad class of channels. For degradable channels in the class, which includes truncated versions of the pure loss Bosonic channel, the resulting scheme achieves the quantum capacity. The construction makes use of polar codes for two kinds of classical-input, quantum-output (CQ) channels: those with commuting outputs and those whose outputs are heralded mixtures of pure states. The former case is amenable to decoding by classical polar decoding algorithms, the latter by the belief propagation with quantum messages (BPQM) algorithm. To our knowledge, this construction is the first explicit and efficiently decodable protocol that achieves the energy-constrained quantum capacity of the pure-loss Bosonic channel.

</details>


### [111] [How I Learned to Stop Worrying and Love the Redfield Equation: Completely-Positive Resummation of Non-Markovian Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03676)
*Greg Kaplanek,Jason Pollack*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文比较了Redfield方程与基于同阶微扰映射的Magnus指数重求和及CPTP resolvent重求和，指出三者到二阶一致但高阶因时间排序修正而不同，并给出弱耦合判据以保证Redfield演化接近CPTP映射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Redfield方程（含时系数TCL2）是开放量子系统微扰动力学最简描述之一，但其近似地位微妙：微扰论逐阶确定约化动力学，而求解Redfield方程对应一种未必保持完全正性的特定重求和。作者希望澄清Redfield演化何时以及为何在高度非Lindblad时仍可靠。

Method: 将Redfield演化与同一二阶微扰映射的指数化（Magnus）重求和进行比较，后者在广泛物理条件下为CPTP；另构造一个简单的CPTP resolvent重求和，以说明同一微扰映射重求和不唯一。通过比较Redfield与Magnus prescription的阶次差异，推导弱耦合判据，并在有限维（qubit）系统中以两个例子说明。

Result: Redfield与Magnus prescription到二阶一致，但在更高阶因时间排序修正而不同；所构造的CPTP resolvent重求和表明重求和不唯一；给出一个弱耦合判据，保证Redfield演化即使在非马尔可夫情形下仍接近CPTP映射，并在两个qubit例子中验证。

Conclusion: 研究澄清了Redfield演化在高度非Lindblad时仍可保持可靠的条件与原因，说明其与CPTP重求和的关系及重求和不唯一性。

Abstract: The Redfield equation with time-dependent coefficients (or TCL2 equation) gives one of the simplest perturbative descriptions of open quantum system dynamics derived from a microscopic theory. Its status as an approximation is nevertheless subtle: while perturbation theory fixes the reduced dynamics order-by-order in the system-environment coupling, solving the Redfield equation yields a particular resummation that need not preserve complete positivity. Here we compare Redfield evolution with an exponentiated (Magnus) resummation of the same second-order perturbative map, which is completely positive and trace preserving (CPTP) for a broad class of physical conditions. We also construct a simple CPTP resolvent resummation, illustrating the non-uniqueness of resummations of the same perturbative map. The Redfield and Magnus prescriptions agree to second order but differ through time-ordering corrections at higher orders. We exhibit a weak-coupling criterion which guarantees that Redfield evolution, even when non-Markovian, remains close to a CPTP map. We illustrate the criterion in two examples, focusing on finite-dimensional (qubit) systems. Our results clarify when, and why, Redfield evolution can remain reliable even when it is highly non-Lindbladian.

</details>


### [112] [Low-Overhead Quantum Error Correction with Boundary-Connected Planar Modules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03682)
*Oscar Higgott,Hasan Sayginel,Francisco J. H. Heras,Zhiyang He,Tomas Jochym-O'Connor,Andrew W. Senior,Lei M. Zhang,Thomas Edlich,James S. Spencer,Matt McEwen,Craig Gidney,Johannes Bausch,Pushmeet Kohli,Hartmut Neven*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出将量子处理器划分为平面模块并通过稀疏、静态的非局域边界连接，构造模块化双曲曲面码与色彩码。电路级仿真表明该模块化存储设计可将物理量子比特开销较表面码降低十倍以上，并在更大系统规模下超越双变量自行车码的速率与距离，为通用容错架构提供高效密集存储方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实用化量子计算机需要量子纠错，但广泛采用的平面表面码存在巨大的物理量子比特开销。作者希望在不牺牲平面模块制造优势的前提下，获得高编码速率、高距离的纠错码。

Method: 通过稀疏、静态的边界连接将欧几里得平面模块互联，基于新的半双曲码族构造模块化双曲曲面码与色彩码；采用模块化伴随式提取电路，并结合神经网络解码器与匹配解码器进行电路级仿真；同时开发基于码自同构的容错行走电路以实现逻辑操作，并与低权重、高对称性逻辑基及比码本身小约4.5倍的模块化提取器系统协同设计。

Result: 在存在较高模块间接缝错误率的情况下，该模块化存储设计仍能将物理量子比特开销较表面码降低十倍或更多。外推至更大系统，编码速率 k/n=1/16、距离 d≥22 的模块化码在速率与距离上均超过 [[288,12,18]] 双Gross双变量自行车码，且主要使用平面模块内的近邻门，隐含相对表面码超过30倍的开销缩减。

Conclusion: 模块化存储可作为通用容错架构中的密集存储单元，在不牺牲平面模块制造优势的同时释放高码率编码的效率，为实用化量子纠错提供可行路径。

Abstract: Realizing practical quantum computers requires quantum error correction, but the most widely implemented approach, the planar surface code, demands a substantial physical qubit overhead. Here, we demonstrate that partitioning quantum processors into manageable, flat modules with non-local inter-module connections provides a natural solution. By wiring sparse, static boundary connections between Euclidean planar modules, we construct modular hyperbolic surface and color codes, based on new families of semi-hyperbolic codes. Using modular syndrome extraction circuits and efficient neural network and matching decoders, our circuit-level simulations show that this modular memory design can reduce the physical qubit overhead by tenfold or more compared to the surface code, even under elevated inter-module seam error rates. Projecting to larger system sizes, we find modular codes with encoding rate $k/n=1/16$ and distance $d\geq 22$, exceeding both the rate and distance of the $[[288,12,18]]$ two-gross bivariate bicycle code while using mostly nearest-neighbor gates within planar modules, and implying an over $30\times$ overhead reduction relative to surface codes. We develop fault-tolerant walking circuits for implementing logic via code automorphisms, which we co-design with low-weight, highly symmetrical logical bases and a modular extractor system that is $\approx 4.5\times$ smaller than the code itself. Combining these methods, our modular memory can be used as dense storage in a universal fault-tolerant architecture, unlocking the efficiency of high-rate codes without sacrificing the fabrication advantages of planar modules.

</details>


### [113] [Single-Shot Error Correction at Optimal Spacetime Cost](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03684)
*Kishor Bharti,Tobias Haug,Derek Khu,Andrew Tanggara*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作构造了显式噪声纠错电路与高效解码方案，在长程连接与快速经典处理硬件假设下，用量子Tanner码实现与最优下界匹配的存储时空代价，可靠性仅增加对数级开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有结果证明独立擦除下存储K个逻辑量子比特、持续S步、误差不超过ε，需要Ω(S(K+log(S/ε)))的时空代价，且已有匹配构造，但该构造假设纠错理想无误。本文旨在去除这一理想化假设，在显式噪声电路与高效解码下达到相同标度。

Method: 采用量子Tanner码；每一步存储包含一轮症候提取，随后固定数量的并行经典解码步，将残余误差降至足够低以保证后续故障仍可纠正；统计所有态制备、门、测量与等待位置；针对足够弱但一般的电路噪声，包括症候提取期间的故障与相关故障。

Result: 在支持长程量子比特连接与快速可靠经典处理的硬件下，达到与最优下界至常数因子匹配的标度；每步失败概率指数小，而每步存储代价与码大小成线性；可靠性仅带来所有被存储量子比特共享的对数级开销；分析可推广至某些常数深度逻辑Clifford操作。

Conclusion: 噪声纠错不会改变存储的渐近时空代价标度，仅引入对数级开销；显式电路与高效解码即可实现最优擦除容错存储，且方法可延伸至有限类别的逻辑门操作。

Abstract: Recent work established that, for independent erasures, storing $K$ logical qubits for $S$ time steps with error at most $\varepsilon$ requires a spacetime cost of $Ω(S(K+\log(S/\varepsilon)))$. A matching construction was also given, but assumes ideal error correction. Here we show that the same scaling can be achieved with an explicit noisy error-correction circuit and efficient decoding, counting all state-preparation, gate, measurement, and wait locations, provided that the hardware supports long-range qubit connectivity and fast, reliable classical processing. Thus, reliability adds only a logarithmic overhead shared by all stored qubits. The construction works for sufficiently weak but otherwise general circuit noise, including faults during syndrome extraction and correlated faults, and for independent erasures it matches the best known lower bound up to constant factors. We attain this scaling using quantum Tanner codes, with each memory step consisting of one round of syndrome extraction followed by a fixed number of parallel classical decoder steps that reduce the residual error enough to keep later faults correctable, regardless of memory size, storage time, or target accuracy. Together, this gives exponentially small failure probability per step while keeping the cost of each memory step linear in the code size. The same analysis also extends to certain constant-depth logical Clifford operations.

</details>


### [114] [Universal Bounds for Out-of-Distribution Unitary Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03690)
*Joachim Favre,Armando Angrisani,Zoë Holmes*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出基于输入分布一阶与二阶矩的分布外量子动力学学习框架，用"偏置"与"表达力"刻画系综性质，并据此控制酉动力学在任意纯态训练/测试分布间的泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心问题是：未知酉动力学在一族状态上的作用，能在多大程度上揭示它在其他状态上的行为？作者希望建立统一的分布外泛化理论框架。

Method: 利用输入分布的一阶矩和二阶矩定义系综的偏置（bias）与表达力（expressivity），以此分析任意纯态训练分布到测试分布之间的泛化。

Result: 仅学习酉在随机实态上的作用即可学到其在随机复态上的作用；无偏纯态系综的张量积保持表达力；单量子比特风险与偏置无关。同时将早期局域scrambling结果作为宽松特例回收，并给出紧的product-to-Haar界。

Conclusion: 给出一系列no-go结果及学习特定信道的有限泛化界，凸显酉性在实现分布外泛化中的独特作用。

Abstract: How much can the action of an unknown unitary dynamics on one family of states reveal about its action elsewhere? We develop a general framework for out-of-distribution quantum dynamics learning based on the first and second moments of the input distribution. These define a notion of the bias and expressivity of an ensemble, which together control generalization between arbitrary pure-state training and testing distributions. Our framework shows that only studying the action of a unitary on random real states can learn its action on random complex states, that tensor products of unbiased pure-state ensembles preserve expressivity, and that single-qubit risks are independent of bias. It also recovers earlier locally scrambling results as loose special cases and yields tight product-to-Haar bounds. We conclude with a series of no-go results and limited generalization bounds for learning certain channels, which further serve to underscore the distinctive role of unitarity in enabling out-of-distribution generalization.

</details>


### [115] [An operational characterization of finite-dimensional quantum theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03694)
*Lionel J. Dmello,Xiangling Xu,Marc-Olivier Renou,David Gross*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文构造了一组有限数量的两体关联，证明唯一能实现这些关联且在迭代量子隐形传态下保持稳定的概率理论就是量子理论，从而为有限维量子理论提供了自检验方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子力学基础的核心目标之一是识别刻画物理理论的操作性约束。Bell不等式刻画了经典概率论，Tsirelson界则迈出了刻画量子力学的第一步。本文关注其对偶问题：能否证明量子理论所预言的所有关联实际上都是可实现的？即量子理论本身能否被自检验。

Method: 作者构造有限数量的两体关联，并要求任何能实现这些关联的理论满足：（1）能实现这组关联；（2）这种实现在迭代量子隐形传态下保持稳定。对(C^d)^{⊗n}体系，条件(1)可用poly(d,n)个测量设置验证；条件(2)可理解为一个测试层级，对应每个隐形传态步数N。

Result: 结果表明，唯一同时满足上述两个条件（即实现这些关联且在迭代隐形传态下稳定）的概率理论是量子理论。这为有限维量子理论提供了自检验的意义，并特别地可以证明存在比任何已直接观测到的都更强的Bell不等式违反。

Conclusion: 有限维量子理论在某种意义上是可自检验的：通过有限数量的两体关联加上迭代隐形传态稳定性条件，可以唯一地认证量子理论。这一框架还能认证超出直接观测范围的Bell不等式违反。

Abstract: A key goal in the foundations of quantum mechanics is to identify operational constraints characterizing physical theories. Bell inequalities do so for classical probability theory, while Tsirelson's bound provides a first step for quantum mechanics. Here, we address the dual question: Can one certify that all correlations predicted by quantum theory are actually realizable? In this work, we construct a finite number of two-body correlations such that the only probabilistic theory that (1) realizes them, and (2) does so in a way that is stable under iterated teleportation, is quantum theory. For $(\mathbb{C}^d)^{\otimes n}$, Condition (1) can be verified using $\operatorname{poly}(d,n)$ measurement settings. Condition (2) may be understood as a hierarchy of tests, one for each number $N$ of teleportation steps. There is thus a sense in which finite-dimensional quantum theory can be self-tested. In particular, one can certify the existence of Bell inequality violations larger than any that have been directly observed.

</details>


### [116] [On The Complexity of Redundancy-Free Quantum Hamiltonians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03697)
*Matthew B. Hastings,Alexander Schmidhuber*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究无冗余哈密顿量的计算复杂度：在 β²d 较小时给出高效经典算法与亚指数量子算法，较大时近似配分函数为 NP-hard，基态能量估计为 QMA-complete。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无冗余哈密顿量（任一哈密顿项出现奇数次的乘积迹为零）自然出现在 Hamiltonian Decoded Quantum Interferometry (Hamiltonian DQI) 中，该框架将量子态制备的难度分为经典解码步骤与制备该无冗余哈密顿量热场双态两个步骤。论文旨在理解这类哈密顿量本身的难度，并刻画 Hamiltonian DQI 中态制备步骤的复杂度。

Method: 引入"反对易玻璃"(anticommutation glass)，其中阻挫纯粹源于反对易关系，使复杂度在给定逆温 β 下依赖于 β²d（d 为反对易图的度）。对小 β²d 给出近似配分函数的高效经典算法；对大的 β²d 证明近似配分函数 NP-hard。给出小 β²d 下制备热场态的亚指数时间量子算法，并基于 Feiguin-Klich 哈密顿量给出多项式时间算法的部分结果。

Result: 无冗余哈密顿量的难度在比一般哈密顿量低二次方的温度处变易；小 β²d 时经典可高效近似配分函数，大 β²d 时近似配分函数 NP-hard。基于 Feiguin-Klich 哈密顿量，得到在度为 d 的相互作用图上、β≲1/d 时制备一般哈密顿量热场双态的多项式时间算法。此外证明无冗余哈密顿量的基态能量估计是 QMA-complete。

Conclusion: 无冗余哈密顿量呈现由反对易关系驱动的独特阻挫结构，其复杂度阈值由 β²d 控制。相关工作既刻画了 Hamiltonian DQI 态制备步骤的复杂度，又独立地给出了热场双态制备的多项式时间算法与基态能量估计的 QMA-complete 结果。

Abstract: We consider a class of redudancy-free Hamiltonians, which are those where the trace vanishes for any product of Hamiltonian terms in which at least one term appears an odd number of times. This includes Hamiltonians which are sums of products of Paulis with no relations between them. These Hamiltonians arise naturally in Hamiltonian Decoded Quantum Interferometry, which separates the hardness of preparing quantum states into two steps: a classical decoding step, and a step preparing the thermofield double state of such a redundancy-free Hamiltonian.
  We focus on the hardness of these Hamiltonians, both for intrinsic interest and to understand the complexity of the state preparation step in Hamiltonian DQI. We present several results showing that the problem becomes easy at quadratically lower temperature than for a general Hamiltonian, with the complexity at given inverse temperature $β$ depending on $β^2 d$, where $d$ is the degree of the anticommutation graph. We give an efficient classical algorithm for small $β^2 d$ to approximate the partition function, while for large $β^2 d$ we show that approximating the partition function is NP-hard. To this end, we introduce what we call an anticommutation glass, where frustration arises purely from anticommutation relations. We give a subexponential time quantum algorithm to prepare the thermofield state for small $β^2 d$, and present some results toward a polynomial time algorithm based on the Feiguin-Klich Hamiltonian. As a result of possible independent interest, we show that this Hamiltonian leads to a polynomial time algorithm for preparing thermofield double states of general Hamiltonians on an interaction graph of degree $d$ for $β\lesssim 1/d$. Finally, we show that estimating the ground state energy of redundancy-free Hamiltonians is QMA-complete.

</details>


### [117] [Exponential lower bounds on the fermionic Gaussian rank of magic states and the bosonic coherent state rank of Fock states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03699)
*Oliver Reardon-Smith*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了费米高斯分解 |M⟩^{⊗k} 至少需要 Ω(1.4^k) 项，并在 δ-近似意义下给出几乎匹配的界；同时确定了 n 模玻色子 Fock 态的相干态边界秩恰为 ∏_j(1+m_j)，验证了相关猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典模拟量子力学的算法运行时间中超多项式部分依赖于将"魔法"态张量积写成"自由"态叠加所需的项数（即秩）。这类秩（如稳定子秩、费米高斯秩）预期随张量因子数指数增长，但已知下界很弱：稳定子秩仅有二次下界，费米高斯秩除固定常数外无任何界。本文旨在给出更强的秩下界。

Method: 针对费米线性光学中最标准的魔法态 |M⟩（可实现交换门的四量子比特态），分析 |M⟩^{⊗k} 的费米高斯分解项数；通过该方法同时推导 δ-近似秩下界，并将精确秩结果推广到任意 k 个固定宇称非高斯态之积；此外用同一方法分析 n 模玻色子 Fock 态的相干态边界秩。

Result: 证明 |M⟩^{⊗k} 的任意费米高斯分解至少含 Ω(1.4^k) 项；δ-近似秩下界为精确秩下界乘以 (1-δ^2)；精确费米高斯秩结果直接适用于 k 个固定宇称非高斯态之积（近似秩不适用）；证明 n 模玻色子 Fock 态（第 j 模含 m_j 个玻色子）的相干态边界秩恰为 ∏_j(1+m_j)，并给出近似相干态秩下界为该量乘以近似保真度的函数。

Conclusion: 本文给出了费米高斯秩的首个超越常数的指数下界，显著推进了魔法态分解复杂度的理解；明确了 Fock 态相干态边界秩的精确表达式并回应了文献猜想；所采用方法具有广泛适用性，可统一处理精确秩与近似秩问题。

Abstract: Recent algorithms for classical simulation of quantum mechanics have runtime whose superpolynomial component is given by a linear dependence on the number of terms required to write large tensor products of certain "magic" states as superpositions of "free" states (which may be stabilizer states, fermionic Gaussian states or others). Surprisingly little is known about the number of terms in such decompositions, called ranks (e.g. the stabilizer rank, fermionic-Gaussian rank etc.). For complexity theoretic reasons they are expected to grow exponentially in the number of tensor factors but, while exponential upper bounds are known for both the stabilizer and fermionic-Gaussian rank of magic states; the best known lower bounds on the stabiliser rank are quadratic, and no bounds on the fermionic-Gaussian rank are known beyond fixed constants. In this work we prove that any fermionic Gaussian decomposition of $\lvert M\rangle^{\otimes k}$ consists of $Ω(1.4^k)$ terms. Here $\lvert M\rangle$ is the most standard magic state for fermionic linear optics: the $4$ qubit state that may be consumed to implement a swap gate. We also prove essentially matching bounds on the $δ$-approximate rank of the same state, lower bounding it by the same quantity that bounds the exact rank, multiplied by a factor of $1-δ^2$. Our results on exact fermionic Gaussian rank apply directly to any product of $k$ fixed parity non-Gaussian states, although the same is not true of the approximate rank. Finally, we prove that the coherent state border rank of an $n$-mode bosonic Fock state with $m_j$ bosons in mode $j$ is exactly $\prod_{j}(1+m_j)$, answering a conjecture of Ref. [1] and obtain lower bounds on the approximate coherent state rank given by the same quantity multiplied by a function of the fidelity of the approximation, emphasizing the broad applicability of the method we employ.

</details>


### [118] [Scalable Passive QRAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03700)
*Siddhartha Jain,Alexander M. Dalzell,Connor T. Hann*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种可扩展、被动式的物理QRAM实现蓝图，原则上证明其可行性。每次查询能耗O(log N)，运行时间O(log² N)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实现物理QRAM操作，并追求可扩展性与被动式设计（低能耗），以降低每个查询的能量成本。

Method: 针对n量子比特地址、N=2^n存储比特的情形，通过演化一个预先制备的、时间无关的4-local Hamiltonian来实现；该Hamiltonian含O(N)个项，每个量子比特最多参与4个项。

Result: 每次QRAM查询能量成本为O(log N)，据作者所知是首个使用polylog N能耗的构造；每次查询运行时间为O(log² N)。

Conclusion: 该构造被动且可扩展，原则上证明了物理QRAM的可行性，并首次达到polylog N级能耗。

Abstract: We present a blueprint for implementing the physical QRAM operation in a scalable, passive manner, proving its feasibility in principle. Our construction is passive because for an $n$-qubit address over $N = 2^n$ memory bits the energy cost per QRAM query is $O(\log N)$, and to our knowledge it is the first construction to use $\operatorname{polylog} N$ energy. The runtime for each query is $O(\log^2 N)$. It is achieved by evolving a pre-manufactured time-independent 4-local Hamiltonian with $O(N)$ terms where every qubit participates in at most 4 terms.

</details>


### [119] [Unitary complexity in polynomial space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03705)
*William Kretschmer,Ewin Tang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文建立了量子承诺存在性与酉合成问题、BPP与NEXP之间的条件性联系，并提出新的酉复杂度类unitaryP和unitaryPSPACE的定义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 若能在无条件情况下证明量子承诺存在，将需要解决复杂性理论中长期悬而未决的问题；量子承诺、酉合成等概念与经典复杂性类之间的精确关系尚不清晰。

Method: 证明一个更一般的引理：unitaryPSPACE中的每个酉算子要么无法相对于任何经典预言机被高效合成，要么可以用NEXP搜索问题的预言机高效合成；并重新定义酉复杂度类unitaryP与unitaryPSPACE，同时考虑擦除垃圾与允许垃圾两种实现。

Result: 若量子承诺存在，则要么酉合成问题无多项式时间解，要么BPP≠NEXP；该引理还蕴含某些涉及unitaryPSPACE的预言机分离可推出NC≠NP等经典下界；并证明unitaryPSPACE可等价刻画为矩阵元可在多项式空间内以任意精度计算的酉变换集合。

Conclusion: 新定义解决了先前工作中的概念问题并带来简洁证明；两种实现定义可互为特例；unitaryPSPACE具有良好鲁棒性，可一般性地擦除垃圾，但该性质相对于酉预言机并不成立。

Abstract: We show that if quantum commitments exist, then either there is no polynomial-time solution to the unitary synthesis problem, or $\mathsf{BPP} \neq \mathsf{NEXP}$. Thus, showing unconditionally that quantum commitments exist would require answering at least one of two longstanding open questions in complexity theory. We prove our main result as a consequence of a more general lemma, which shows that every unitary in $\mathsf{unitaryPSPACE}$ either cannot be synthesized efficiently relative to any classical oracle, or can be synthesized efficiently with an oracle for $\mathsf{NEXP}$ search problems. Our lemma has other noteworthy consequences, including that certain oracle separations involving $\mathsf{unitaryPSPACE}$ would imply breakthrough classical lower bounds such as $\mathsf{NC} \neq \mathsf{NP}$.
  Along the way, we propose new definitions for the unitary complexity classes $\mathsf{unitaryP}$ and $\mathsf{unitaryPSPACE}$. Our changes address the biggest conceptual issues with definitions suggested in prior work, and lead to elegant proofs. We study both implementations that erase garbage and implementations that allow it, because we cannot rule out the possibility that the two definitions differ. Nevertheless, we show that both definitions can be viewed as special cases of each other. We also showcase many other ways in which our definitions are robust. For example, we show that $\mathsf{unitaryPSPACE}$ has an equivalent characterization as the set of unitary transformations whose entries can be computed to arbitrary precision in polynomial space. Consequently, we deduce that $\mathsf{unitaryPSPACE}$ can generically erase garbage, a result that provably fails relative to unitary oracles.

</details>


### [120] [Quantum estimation, channel orders, and private capacity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03706)
*Christoph Hirche*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过二元估计将熵型信道序与近期私有容量激活构造联系起来：测得χ²散度控制最小均方误差，其积分表示可从量子比特扩展上的测得χ²恢复量子χ²，进而得到完整量子χ²、相对熵与less-noisy序的刻画；Hermitian平滑测得χ²序刻画了签名提升的存在性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期结果表明零私有容量并不意味着反退化性，且两个零私有容量信道仍可协同传输私有信息，这挑战了既有熵型信道序对容量的界定。作者希望理解这些激活现象为何发生，以及完整比较与正则化比较在此中的不同作用。

Method: 以二元估计为桥梁，使用由测得χ²散度控制的最小均方误差；给出积分表示，将量子比特扩展上的测得χ²恢复到量子χ²；引入Hermitian平滑测得χ²序；并利用参考辅助的测得χ²见证量分析激活构造。

Result: 得到从完整测得χ²序到完整量子χ²、相对熵及less-noisy序的过渡；Hermitian平滑测得χ²序刻画签名提升的存在性（即使有量子参考）；每个块长下的环境支配给出有限码可靠性—保密性界；对噪声Werner–Holevo信道确定尖锐的私有容量与反退化阈值，并在非退化区间计算精确互补容量；将Pauli半擦除激活扩展到所有1/2≤p<1并给出新的四能级族；参考辅助测得χ²见证表明激活构造缺乏完整比较。

Conclusion: 完整比较可阻止与反退化辅助者产生激活，而仅正则化比较不能；测得χ²序框架统一解释了零私有容量激活、反退化性及有限码保密界，并为多种信道族给出尖锐阈值与精确容量。

Abstract: Recent examples have shown that zero private capacity need not imply antidegradability and that two channels with zero private capacity can nevertheless transmit private information together. Existing entropic channel orders compare what a receiver and its environment can learn and thereby bound capacities. We connect these orders to the recent constructions through binary estimation, whose minimum mean-square error is governed by measured $χ^2$ divergence. A new integral representation shows how measured $χ^2$ on a qubit extension recovers quantum $χ^2$. It lets us pass from complete measured-$χ^2$ ordering to complete quantum-$χ^2$, relative-entropy, and less-noisy ordering. Zhu and Wang used a signed lift to show that their qutrit channel has zero private capacity. We show that Hermitian-smoothed measured-$χ^2$ ordering characterizes when such lifts exist, even with a quantum reference. Environmental dominance in binary estimation at every blocklength yields a finite-code reliability--secrecy bound. These results explain why complete comparison prevents activation with antidegradable helpers whereas regularized comparison alone does not. Building on that qutrit example, we establish this stability for a range of noisy Werner--Holevo channels, determine sharp private-capacity and antidegradability thresholds, and compute exact complementary capacities in a nondegradable range. By contrast, we extend Pauli half-erasure activation to all $1/2\le p<1$ and establish the same range for a new four-level family. Reference-assisted measured-$χ^2$ witnesses show why these activating constructions lack complete comparison.

</details>


### [121] [How to Build Pseudorandom Unitaries in Microcrypt](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03711)
*Aditya Gulati,Dakshita Khurana,Kabir Tomer*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文首次给出相对于经典预言机的证据，表明伪随机酉（PRU）可以在不存在量子可计算单向函数的情况下存在：在经典随机预言机模型中构造了能抵抗QMA辅助对手的强PRU，从而形成黑盒分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作（如Kretschmer、Qian、Tal，STOC 2025）仅给出经典预言机下的相关结果，但其安全性未覆盖QMA辅助对手。本文旨在强化这一结果，并探讨PRU与量子可计算单向函数之间的蕴含关系，即PRU是否必然推出量子可计算单向函数。

Method: 首先证明由五个独立随机对角相位层与Hadamard变换交替构成的酉 U=F5HF4HF3HF2HF1 是强PRU，在自适应、受控访问该酉及其逆、转置和复共轭下安全。随后将相位层用经典随机预言机O实现，证明该构造对具有对经典输入QMA^{PH}^O决策问题的合适布尔完备化进行相干访问的一致BQP对手仍然安全。

Result: 构造在经典随机预言机下对可反演（甚至量子可计算）单向函数、并可求解相对于O定义的高效可验证单向谜题的QMA辅助对手保持安全，从而以黑盒分离形式证明PRU不蕴含量子可计算单向函数。该结果显著强于Kretschmer、Qian、Tal（STOC 2025）的经典预言机结果。

Conclusion: 本文提供了经典预言机证据，表明PRU的存在不必然意味着量子可计算单向函数存在。所提构造仅需一个简单高效、查询随机函数的电路，暗示可经由随机预言机启发式实现具体的PRU方案。

Abstract: We provide the first evidence relative to a classical oracle that pseudorandom unitaries (PRUs) can exist without quantum-computable one-way functions.
  To obtain this result, we first prove that five independent random diagonal phase layers interleaved with Hadamard transforms \[ U=F_5HF_4HF_3HF_2HF_1 \] form a strong PRU with security under adaptive, controlled access to the unitary, its inverse, transpose, and complex conjugate.
  Our main result is that, when the phase layers are implemented using a classical random oracle O, the construction remains secure against uniform BQP adversaries with coherent access to suitable Boolean completions of classical-input $QMA^{PH}^{O}}$ decision problems. Such an adversary can invert (even quantum-computable) one-way functions and solve efficiently verifiable one-way puzzles defined relative to O. Our result therefore provides classical-oracle evidence that PRUs do not imply quantum-computable one-way functions, in the form of a black-box separation.
  We substantially strengthen the classical-oracle result of Kretschmer, Qian, and Tal (STOC 2025) by obtaining PRUs secure against these QMA-aided adversaries. Our construction also consists of a simple, efficient circuit that queries a random function, suggesting a route to concrete implementation via the random-oracle heuristic.

</details>


### [122] [SpiderCSS: Scalable Fault-Tolerant CSS State Preparation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03714)
*Boldizsár Poór,Andrey Boris Khesin,Sarah Meng Li,Benjamin Rodatz,John van de Wetering,Richie Yeung*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出SpiderCSS编译流程，利用ZX演算与容错等价重写为任意CSS码生成高度优化的容错态制备电路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 容错逻辑态制备是实现大规模容错量子计算的关键原语，现有可扩展/启发式方法在电路深度、量子比特占用及逻辑错误率上仍有优化空间。

Method: 从CSS态的理想化规范出发，使用ZX演算和容错等价重写构造制备目标态且保持初始理想ZX图容错性的图；再用模块化构造生成容错CSS态制备电路，各组件内部及组件互连均实现CNOT最小化；仅用容错等价重写，无需昂贵验证即可证明容错性。

Result: 对距离至多15的多种CSS码的蒙特卡洛模拟表明，SpiderCSS可高效扩展，电路深度与量子比特占用显著低于现有可扩展和启发式方法，逻辑错误率平均降低33.7%，接收率平均提高9.5%。

Conclusion: SpiderCSS提供了一种可扩展、可证明容错且高效的CSS态制备电路编译方案，在性能上优于已有方法。

Abstract: Fault-tolerant preparation of logical states is a critical primitive for the realization of large-scale fault-tolerant quantum computing. This paper introduces SpiderCSS, a scalable compilation pipeline that generates highly-optimized, fault-tolerant state preparation circuits for arbitrary Calderbank-Shor-Steane (CSS) codes. Starting from an idealized specification of a CSS state, we use the ZX-calculus and fault-equivalent rewrites to construct a diagram that prepares the given state while preserving the fault tolerance of the initial ideal ZX-diagram. From this diagram, SpiderCSS constructs a fault-tolerant CSS-state preparation circuit using a modular construction that is CNOT-minimal both within each component and also in the way these components are connected together. By only using fault-equivalent rewrites, the final circuit is provably fault-tolerant without needing expensive verification. Monte Carlo simulations across a variety of CSS codes up to distance 15 show that SpiderCSS scales efficiently and constructs circuits with substantially reduced depth and qubit footprint compared to existing scalable and heuristic methods, while simultaneously achieving an average decrease of 33.7% in logical error rate and an increase of 9.5% in acceptance rate.

</details>
