### [123] [Breakdown of material-time scaling in the microscopic dynamics of metallic glasses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02426)
*Till Böhmer,Jie Shen,Jan Philipp Gabriel,Rolf Zeißler,Federico Zontone,Fan Yang,Eloi Pineda,Beatrice Ruta,Thomas Blochowicz*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 金属玻璃的物理老化中，材料时间能描述平均结构性质的演化，但微观密度-密度自相关函数严重违反材料时间标度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 判断材料时间（material time）概念是否也适用于金属玻璃的微观动力学。金属玻璃与平衡过冷液体的微观动力学在定性上不同，这一问题此前不清楚。

Method: 对处于平衡态的金属玻璃施加小温度跳变，利用同步时间分辨X射线衍射与X射线光子相关谱，追踪其完整弛豫过程；并用Tool-Narayanaswamy-Moynihan（TNM）模型进行定量描述。

Result: 第一尖锐衍射峰位置随时间的演化呈现温度上/下跳变的不对称性，符合材料时间老化特征，并可被TNM模型定量描述。相反，微观密度-密度自相关函数严重违反材料时间标度：即使温度变化小至4K，无论跳变方向如何，都出现压缩指数弛豫，并随平均结构平衡化而持续演化为拉伸指数弛豫形状。

Conclusion: 材料时间可描述平均结构性质的演化，即使相应的微观动力学不服从材料时间标度。压缩指数行为与体积受挫导致的瞬态应力介导贡献一致，在平衡后消失。

Abstract: Physical aging in glasses is often described using the concept of material time, which assumes the underlying relaxation mechanism to remain the same as in equilibrium, but with a rate that changes during aging. Whether material time also captures the microscopic dynamics of metallic glasses, which differ qualitatively between the glass and the equilibrium supercooled liquid, is unclear. Using simultaneous time-resolved X-ray diffraction and X-ray photon-correlation spectroscopy, we follow the full equilibration of a metallic glass after small temperature jumps starting from equilibrium. The time evolution of the first sharp diffraction peak position exhibits the asymmetry between temperature up- and down-jumps characteristic of material-time aging and is quantitatively described by the Tool-Narayanaswamy-Moynihan model, which is based on this concept. By contrast, the microscopic density-density autocorrelation functions strongly violate material-time scaling. Compressed-exponential relaxation emerges even after temperature changes as small as 4K, irrespective of the direction of the temperature jump, and continuously evolves into a stretched-exponential relaxation shape following the equilibration of the average structure. This behavior is consistent with a transient stress-mediated contribution associated with volumetric frustration that disappears upon equilibration. Our results show that a material time can describe the evolution of average structural properties even when the corresponding microscopic dynamics do not obey material-time scaling.

</details>


### [124] [Universal energy barrier for plastic flow in a network glass](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02454)
*Ibrahim Ghanem,Richard Jana,Wolfram G. Nöhring,Michael Moseler,Lars Pastewka*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 该论文利用机器学习原子间势，揭示非晶碳中的剪切转变对应单个共价键的断裂或形成，其反应坐标为键长；尽管存在结构无序，所有能量势垒曲线可归约为同一普适形状，仅由键跳距离这一单尺度参数决定，并由此建立从键级物理量预测塑性流动应力的模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 剪切转变是玻璃塑性的基本事件，支撑了许多介观塑性流动模型，但其原子起源和能量学特征一直难以确立。

Method: 采用机器学习原子间势（machine-learned interatomic potential）模拟非晶碳（代表性网络玻璃），以键长作为反应坐标，提取剪切转变的能量景观与势垒，并分析其激活统计。

Result: 剪切转变对应单个共价键的断裂或形成；所有能量景观坍缩为普适势垒形状，仅键跳距离这一尺度参数通过立方律决定势垒高度；结合激活统计可由独立的键级量预测塑性流动应力。

Conclusion: 研究结果为玻璃塑性流动的唯象模型提供了微观支持，并指出了实现非晶塑性定量描述的可能路径。

Abstract: Shear transformations are the elementary events of plasticity in glasses and underpin many mesoscopic models of plastic flow, yet their atomic origin and energetics remain difficult to establish. Here we use a machine-learned interatomic potential to show that, in amorphous carbon as a representative network glass, a shear transformation corresponds to the breaking or formation of a single covalent bond. The simplicity of the associated reaction coordinate, the bond length, enables us to extract energy landscapes and barriers. Despite the intrinsic structural disorder, we find that all energy landscapes collapse onto a universal barrier shape. What remains bond-specific is a single scale, the bond-jump distance, which fixes the barrier height through a cubic law. Combined with the activation statistics of shear transformations, this yields a model that predicts the plastic flow stress from independently determined bond-level quantities. Our results provide microscopic support for phenomenological models of plastic flow in glasses and suggest a route towards a quantitative description of amorphous plasticity.

</details>


### [125] [Quantum transmission in 1D disordered stealthy hyperuniform Kronig--Penney-like models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02501)
*Shaobing Yuan,Carlo Vanoni,Salvatore Torquato*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 首次为一维连续点散射体构成的隐身高斯超均匀(SHU)无序系统建立量子输运微扰理论，证明Fourier空间隐身条件使低阶项精确抵消，实空间超均匀约束进一步压低Lyapunov指数四阶项。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SHU无序系统因隐身区域内单散射贡献消失，被视为调控经典与量子输运的平台；然而此前尚缺乏针对一维连续点散射体SHU无序的量子输运微扰理论，需要从理论层面揭示波传播中SHU结构的作用机制。

Method: 构建一维量子输运微扰理论，研究对象为具有SHU位置无序的连续相同点散射体；基于Fourier空间的隐身条件S(q)=0与实空间超均匀约束lim_{D→∞}σ_D²=c<∞，对Lyapunov指数λ(k)作逐阶展开分析。

Result: 微扰理论显示所有低阶项因隐身条件S(q)=0而精确抵消；最低非零的四阶项在Lyapunov指数λ(k)中也被进一步抑制，其抑制源于实空间超均匀约束lim_{D→∞}σ_D²=c<∞。

Conclusion: 该结果揭示了无序SHU体系中一般波传播现象的普适性含义，为利用超均匀结构调控经典与量子输运提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Disordered stealthy hyperuniform (SHU) systems are emerging as platforms for controlling classical and quantum transport due to vanishing single-scattering contributions in the stealthy regime. Here, to our knowledge, we present the first perturbation theory of one-dimensional quantum transport through a continuum of identical point scatterers with SHU positional disorder. Our perturbation theory indicates the exact cancellation of all the lower-order terms due to the Fourier-space stealthy condition $S(q)=0$, and the further suppression of the lowest non-vanishing fourth order in the Lyapunov exponent $λ(k)$ due to the real-space hyperuniform constraint $\lim\limits_{D\to\infty}σ_D^2=c<\infty$. Our findings presents universal implications of the general wave-propagation phenomena in disordered SHU systems.

</details>


### [126] [Cup and Cap Topological Neural Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03169)
*Marta Niedostatek,Ferran Hernandez Caralt,Runyue Wang,Federica Baccini,Lorenzo Giambagli,Pietro Lió,Ginestra Bianconi*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文提出采用上积（cup）与下积（cap）乘积，构建 Cup 和 Cap 拓扑神经网络（CCNN），突破传统方法每层仅能跨一维升降的局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有拓扑深度学习（TDL）方法大多基于边界算子和 Hodge 拉普拉斯算子，其特征与信号在每层中无法跨越多于一个维度进行升维或降维，限制了高阶单纯形（边、三角形等）与节点之间的多体交互建模。

Method: 引入代数拓扑中的 cup 与 cap 乘积，将其作为跨维度操作，构建 CCNN 架构；该架构以 0-链（节点变量）为学习目标，同时融入数据中存在的多体交互（如三角形）。

Result: 在 TopoBench 数据集上进行评估，CCNN 表现出与其他单纯复形神经网络相当的竞争性能。

Conclusion: cup/cap 乘积为 TDL 提供了跨多维度信息传递的新机制，CCNN 验证了该思路的有效性，可作为单纯复形神经网络的有力替代方案。

Abstract: Topological Deep Learning (TDL) is designed to learn features associated with higher-dimensional simplices including not only nodes but also edges, triangles and so on. However, most TDL approaches, being based on boundary operators and Hodge Laplacians, have the limitation that features and signals cannot be lifted or lowered across more than one dimension per layer. To overcome this limitation, in this work, we propose the adoption of the cup and cap products. Specifically, we formulate the Cup and Cap Topological Neural Network (CCNN), a topological deep learning architecture designed to learn node-based variables (or 0-cochains) by taking into account their many-body interactions (e.g. triangles) present in the data. We validate CCNN by assessing its performance on the TopoBench datasets, revealing its competitiveness with respect to other simplicial complex neural networks.

</details>


### [127] [Discrete Scale Invariance of Ising Mesons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35940)
*Denis V. Vasilyev,Abbas Ali Saberi*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 该文证明离散尺度不变性（DSI）可在有序量子磁体内部的复合激发运动中出现，由长程键导致的渐近平方反比吸引（强度由自发磁化固定）与双扭结阈值曲率（相对动能标度）共同决定尺度反常指数及介子层级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散尺度不变性此前多被认为源于共振少体动力学或奇异尺度不变动力学，作者希望寻找一种新的、微观多体层面的实现途径，即在有序量子磁体的复合激发的内禀运动中出现。

Method: 研究对象为边缘$1/r^4$横场Ising链；长程键在“着装”畴壁间产生渐近平方反比吸引，其强度由自发磁化固定；双扭结阈值曲率设定相对动能标度。采用全自旋精确对角化，并结合四阶弱场哈密顿量进行分析。

Result: 尺度反常指数由两个独立可及的物理量确定，进而决定介子层级；超临界区域中同一指数支配几何结合能与尺寸比、对数能级累积以及对数周期阈值散射；数值结果支持有限窗口内能量-尺寸的耦合标度行为。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了量子极限环物理的微观多体实现，其普适标度由磁有序与扭结迁移率共同固定。

Abstract: Discrete scale invariance often arises from resonant few-body or singular scale-invariant dynamics. We show that it can instead emerge in the internal motion of a composite excitation in an ordered quantum magnet. In the marginal $1/r^4$ transverse-field Ising chain, long-range bonds generate an asymptotic inverse-square attraction between dressed domain walls, with strength fixed by the spontaneous magnetization. The dressed two-kink threshold curvature sets the relative kinetic scale. Together, these independently accessible quantities determine the scale-anomaly exponent and hence the meson hierarchy. In the supercritical regime, the same exponent governs geometric binding-energy and size ratios, logarithmic level accumulation, and log-periodic threshold scattering. Full-spin exact diagonalization and a fourth-order weak-field Hamiltonian support the coupled energy--size scaling in the finite window between core and ring effects. This provides a microscopic many-body realization of quantum limit-cycle physics, with its universal scaling fixed by magnetic order and kink mobility.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.mes-hall'></div>

# cond-mat.mes-hall [[Back]](#toc)

### [128] [Fluctuation-induced magnetoresistance in graphene Hall bars at charge neutrality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02326)
*Anna Grigoreva,Alex Levchenko,Eleftherios Kirkinis,Anton Andreev*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 在石墨烯电荷中性点，电子-电子碰撞主导时电子液体呈流体动力学行为，热涨落使电荷输运与流体流动耦合，从而增强宏观电导并产生正磁阻；本文建立了该涨落机制的定量理论并给出石墨烯霍尔条中磁阻的解析表达式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在T~50-100 K温区，石墨烯器件中电子-电子碰撞率可超过由无序和电子-声子散射决定的动量弛豫率，使电子液体进入流体动力学区。在电荷中性点，纯流体动力学近似下电流与流体流动解耦，电导率仅等于电子液体的本征电导率σ0。近期研究表明，热涨落引起的电荷输运与流体流动耦合会增强宏观电导并在较弱磁场下产生很强的正磁阻，但该机制缺乏定量理论描述。

Method: 在电荷中性点发展磁阻涨落机制的定量理论，推导石墨烯霍尔条中磁阻的解析表达式；并考虑动量弛豫对磁阻的影响。

Result: 获得了石墨烯霍尔条中磁阻的解析表达式。磁阻对磁场H的函数依赖关系敏感于霍尔条的几何形状及其相对于电流的取向；同时定量纳入了动量弛豫对磁阻的效应。

Conclusion: 热涨落导致的电荷输运与流体动力学流动耦合是石墨烯电荷中性点正磁阻的重要机制；所得解析结果给出了磁阻随磁场、霍尔条几何与取向的变化规律，并说明动量弛豫的影响。

Abstract: In the temperature interval $T\sim 50 - 100 K$, the rate of electron-electron collisions in graphene devices may exceed the momentum relaxation rate due to disorder and electron-phonon scattering. In this regime, the motion of the electron liquid may be described by the hydrodynamic equations. In the hydrodynamic approximation, at the charge neutrality point the electric current is decoupled from the hydrodynamic flow, and the conductivity of the system is equal to the intrinsic conductivity of the electron liquid, $σ_0$. It was recently shown that the coupling of charge transport and hydrodynamic flow induced by the thermal fluctuations enhances the macroscopic conductivity of the system and results in very strong positive magnetoresistance (MR) at relatively weak fields. Here we develop a quantitative theory of the fluctuation mechanism of MR at charge neutrality and obtain analytic expressions for MR in graphene Hall bars. The functional dependence of MR on the magnetic field $H$ is sensitive to the Hall bar geometry and orientation relative to the current. We also account for the effect of momentum relaxation on MR.

</details>


### [129] [Chiral Vacuum Engineering of Quantum Hall Matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02400)
*Kiran M. Kulkarni,Andrey Baydin,Motoaki Bamba,Junichiro Kono,Ceren B. Dag,Vasil Rokaj*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 手性腔可区分二维电子气与腔真空的共振/反共振相互作用：共旋偏振下模式反交叉、呈现真空Rabi劈裂并保持裸真空基态；反旋偏振下模式交叉、获得真空诱导能移与双模压缩基态。该现象源于相应哈密顿量的SU(2)与SU(1,1)对称性，并带来手性相关的霍尔与纵向磁阻修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究腔的手性（chirality）如何影响二维电子气与腔真空之间的光-物质相互作用，区分共振与反共振耦合通道，并寻找可观测的输运与光谱学特征。

Method: 理论分析二维电子气与手性腔真空的耦合哈密顿量，利用SU(2)与SU(1,1)对称性框架解释共旋（co-rotating）与反旋（counter-rotating）偏振下的不同行为；提出手性光子晶体腔中的光谱学探测方案，并计算有限极化激元展宽下的霍尔电阻率修正及纵向Shubnikov–de Haas振荡响应；基于近期实现的太赫兹手性腔参数进行估算。

Result: 共旋偏振时模式反交叉并出现真空Rabi劈裂，基态仍为裸真空；反旋偏振时模式交叉，出现真空诱导能移和双模压缩基态。手性腔对霍尔电阻率产生依赖手性的修正（非手性腔无此效应），纵向电阻率的Shubnikov–de Haas振荡对不同旋向呈非对称响应，并给出通过电流涨落探测压缩基态的可行途径。

Conclusion: 腔手性为调控光-物质真空相互作用提供了新自由度，其效应在现有太赫兹手性腔实验条件下可望被观测。

Abstract: We reveal that a chiral cavity distinguishes the resonant and antiresonant interactions between a two-dimensional electron gas and the cavity vacuum. For co-rotating helicities, the modes anticross, exhibiting vacuum Rabi splitting, and retain a bare vacuum ground state. For counter-rotating helicities, the modes cross, acquiring vacuum-mediated energy shifts and a two-mode squeezed ground state. We trace the origin of these observations to the SU(2) and SU(1,1) symmetries of the respective Hamiltonians and present their spectroscopic signatures in chiral photonic crystal cavities. We further predict that cavity chirality produces a handedness-dependent correction to Hall resistivity at finite polariton broadening, while such a correction is not present in an achiral cavity. For the longitudinal resistivity, we find an asymmetric response of the Shubnikov--de Haas oscillations for the different helicities. Finally, we present an experimentally accessible route for detecting the squeezed ground state through current fluctuations. Parameters based on a recently demonstrated terahertz chiral cavity place these effects within experimental reach.

</details>


### [130] [Highly Tunable Photon-Magnon Coupling Governed by Collective-Mode Profiles in Reconfigurable Dielectric-Resonator Arrays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02422)
*Shihao Zhou,Edward Kwao,Junming Wu,Abhijit Shinde,Yin Lyu,Rene Lopez,Wencan Jin,Yi Li,Binbin Yang,Wei Zhang*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 在共同微带线上的模块化介质谐振器阵列中实现了位点选择性的磁子-光子耦合，证明耦合强度由集体光子模式的局域微波磁场幅度（而非仅谱共振）决定，节点处无分裂。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微波光子模式的空间结构为调控其与局域磁激发的相互作用提供了额外自由度；本文旨在实现可后制造重构的、位点选择性的磁子-光子杂化界面。

Method: 将离散介质谐振器以模块化阵列形式安装在共用微带线上，可在同一PCB上增删或重排谐振器以实现几何重构；依次将YIG小球置于各谐振器上方，用面内磁场调节其Kittel模扫过集体光子共振；结合全波仿真与耦合模模型分析。

Result: 三谐振器阵列中，局域磁场非零的模式在每一测量位点均与磁子杂化；而含中心场节点的模式在小球位于该节点时无可分辨劈裂。五谐振器阵列成功验证了该局域节点选择规则，预测的位点依赖耦合图样与测量一致。

Conclusion: 该工作确立了磁子-光子空间杂化的后制造调控手段，为未来混合量子微波架构中的可重构磁子界面提供了途径。

Abstract: The spatial structure of a microwave photon mode provides a degree of freedom for controlling its interaction with a localized magnetic excitation. We demonstrate site-selective magnon-photon coupling in modular arrays of discrete dielectric resonators mounted on a common microstrip line. The resonators can be added, removed, or rearranged on the same completed PCB, enabling post-fabrication geometrical reconfiguration of the collective photon modes and their nodal structure. A YIG sphere is positioned successively above individual resonators, while an in-plane magnetic field tunes its Kittel mode through the collective photon resonances. In a three-resonator array, modes with finite local microwave magnetic fields hybridize with the magnon at every measured site, whereas a mode containing a central field node exhibits no resolvable splitting when the sphere is positioned at that node. Full-wave simulations and a coupled-mode model show that the interaction is governed by the local amplitude of the collective photon mode rather than by spectral resonance alone. Extending this local-node selection rule to a five-resonator array successfully predicts the measured position-dependent coupling patterns. These results establish post-fabrication control of spatial magnon-photon hybridization and provide a route toward reconfigurable magnonic interfaces in future hybrid quantum microwave architectures.

</details>


### [131] [Magnetic-field response of generalized Wigner crystals in twisted MoTe$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02493)
*F. K. Wolff,S. Jiao,H. Park,J. Cai,E. Anderson,X. Liu,C. Wang,T. Taniguchi,K. Watanabe,X. Xu,D. Xiao,S. Vaitiekėnas*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文报道了层极化扭转MoTe₂双分子层中分数空穴填充态的磁输运研究，发现1/3与2/3填充的对磁场响应截然相反，并指认其为广义Wigner晶体；半填充附近观察到与条纹相一致的各向异性输运。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索摩尔超晶格中分数填充的电荷有序态（如广义Wigner晶体、条纹相）在外加垂直磁场下的输运行为，厘清不同电荷构型与磁性/自旋极化之间的耦合机制，并为摩尔电荷有序态的磁调控提供实验依据。

Method: 对层极化扭转MoTe₂同质双分子层进行磁输运测量，施加垂直磁场，追踪1/3、2/3及半填充附近的电阻峰随磁场的变化（峰高、峰位随磁场的依赖关系）以及输运各向异性。

Result: 垂直磁场抑制2/3填充的电阻峰，却将1/3填充峰增强数个数量级，且两峰均保持在公度密度附近；半填充附近出现性质不同的态，其电阻极大值随磁场近似二次方移动，并表现出与条纹相一致的显著输运各向异性。

Conclusion: 磁输运强烈依赖于分数填充，这些公度态可理解为电荷构型不同、对应不同磁格子的广义Wigner晶体，从而对自旋极化呈现相反响应；该结果为摩尔电荷有序态的磁调控输运开辟了途径。

Abstract: We report a magnetotransport study of fractional hole-filling states in the layer-polarized regime of a twisted MoTe$_2$ homobilayer. A perpendicular magnetic field suppresses the resistance peak at the two-thirds filling while enhancing the one-third peak by several orders of magnitude. Both remain near their commensurate densities. We argue that these commensurate states are generalized Wigner crystals whose distinct charge configurations define different magnetic lattices, leading to contrasting responses to spin polarization. Near half filling, we observe a qualitatively different state whose resistance maximum shifts approximately quadratically with field and exhibits pronounced transport anisotropy consistent with a stripe phase. These results reveal a strong dependence of magnetotransport on fractional filling and open a route to magnetic control of transport in moiré charge-ordered states.

</details>


### [132] [Quantum materials QED with van der Waals crystals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02514)
*Suheng Xu,Brian Vermilyea,Ran Jing,Lukas Wehmeier,Jihoon Park,Rafael A. Mayer,Xinzhong Chen,Samuel L. Moore,Fuyang Tay,Boyi Zhou,Wenjun Zheng,Dihao Sun,Birui Yang,Cory R. Dean,Daniel Cazorla,Xu Du,Milan Delor,Andrew J. Millis,Angel Rubio,Michael M. Fogler,Mengkun Liu,D. N. Basov*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文提出由石墨烯与六方氮化硼(hBN)堆叠构成的范德华多层腔，通过磁场调节石墨烯带间朗道能级跃迁与hBN声子共振，实现中红外宽带、模式选择的强耦合，并预言可达到超强耦合区。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 材料量子电动力学(materials QED)的核心目标是在工程化腔中利用亚波长电磁局域化来调控光-物质相互作用。现有体系在同时实现可调性、多模控制与强耦合方面仍受限，需要一种可扩展平台来灵活调控电磁共振杂化。

Method: 构建由堆叠石墨烯与六方氮化硼组成的范德华多层腔；利用磁场调节石墨烯的带间朗道能级跃迁(ILTs)，使其与hBN声子共振；建立量子材料QED理论框架，分析腔体几何结构与各层放置位置对耦合强度和模式选择性的联合调控作用。

Result: 观测到中红外波段的宽带、模式选择性强耦合；理论表明腔几何与层位置共同决定耦合强度与模态选择性；在实验可行的腔参数下，预测的模式劈裂超过hBN Reststrahlen带宽，为进入超强耦合区提供直接途径。

Conclusion: 范德华多层腔可作为可扩展的可调多模QED平台，拓展了可重构量子材料的设计空间，并将磁调控、声子与朗道能级杂化统一于量子材料QED框架内。

Abstract: A central goal of the emerging field of materials QED is to harness subwavelength electromagnetic confinement in engineered cavities to tailor light-matter interactions. Here, we demonstrate that van der Waals multilayer cavities composed of stacked graphene and hexagonal boron nitride (hBN) provide unprecedented control over hybridization between their electromagnetic resonances. By magnetically tuning graphene inter-Landau-level transitions (ILTs) into resonance with hBN phonons, we witness broadband, mode-selective strong coupling in the mid-infrared. Our quantum-material QED framework shows that cavity geometry and layer placement jointly control both coupling strength and modal selectivity. For experimentally realistic cavity parameters, the predicted mode splitting exceeds the width of the hBN Reststrahlen band, providing a direct route to the ultrastrong coupling regime. Together, these results establish van der Waals multilayer cavities as a scalable platform for tunable multimode QED and broaden the design space for reconfigurable quantum materials.

</details>


### [133] [Extracting the anyon charge from shot noise in complex fractional quantum Hall edges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02818)
*Jinhong Park,Christian Sp{\ra}nsl{ä}tt*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 针对ν=2/3分数量子霍尔边缘的多通道特性，建立包含隧穿散粒噪声与通道间平衡/加热噪声的统一理论框架，提出抑制加热贡献并恢复准粒子电荷的关联组合方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子点接触散粒噪声测量可探测Laughlin分数量子霍尔态中的准粒子电荷，但在多通道边缘中，点接触隧穿噪声与通道间平衡及加热产生的噪声共存，使测量解释变得复杂，需要区分二者的理论框架。

Method: 构建同时包含本征分配噪声与平衡诱导加热贡献的一般理论框架，并应用于典型ν=2/3 FQH边缘；在四端QPC几何下推导漏极自关联与交叉关联的表达式。

Result: 发现平衡诱导加热对自关联和交叉关联的影响在参数依赖上与本征分配噪声不同，据此提出一种特定关联组合，可强烈抑制加热贡献并完整恢复隧穿准粒子电荷。

Conclusion: 该框架为复杂FQH边缘中的散粒噪声测量提供系统解释方法，并可推广至更一般的反向传播边缘结构。

Abstract: Shot-noise measurements with a quantum point contact provide a direct probe of the quasiparticle charge in Laughlin fractional quantum Hall (FQH) states. However, the interpretation of such measurements becomes more subtle for multichannel edges, where the point-contact tunneling noise coexists with noise generated by intermode equilibration and heating. We develop a general framework that incorporates both these contributions and apply it to the paradigmatic $ν=2/3$ FQH edge. We derive expressions for the drain auto- and cross-correlations in a four-terminal QPC geometry and show that equilibration-induced heating affects the auto- and cross-correlations in parametrically different way than the intrinsic partition noise. This distinction permits us to propose a specific combination of the two correlations which strongly suppresses the heating contribution and fully recovers the tunneling quasiparticle charge. Our results thereby provide a systematic framework for interpreting shot-noise measurements in complex FQH edges and can be further extended to more general counterpropagating edge structures.

</details>


### [134] [Topological insulators on complex networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02888)
*Sunkyu Yu,Xianji Piao,Namkyoo Park*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文将拓扑绝缘体从周期晶格推广到一般复杂网络，提出多拓扑目标无冲突设计框架，在Hofstadter晶格及规则、小世界、随机网络中实现拓扑绝缘相，揭示网络特有的拓扑特性与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统拓扑绝缘体研究局限于短程跃迁的周期晶格，虽已有非欧几何、无序结构、长程跃迁等扩展，但真实复杂网络能否自身承载拓扑绝缘相尚不明确，且缺乏多拓扑目标协同设计的框架。

Method: 发展面向多个拓扑目标的无冲突设计框架，在高阶Hofstadter晶格以及规则网络、小世界网络和随机网络中构造并实现拓扑绝缘体。

Result: 成功在复杂网络中实现拓扑绝缘相，发现网络特有的新特性：可获得的拓扑特征范围扩大、随度依赖的从Hofstadter型到Haldane型Chern绝缘体的转变，以及小世界拓扑绝缘体具有更优的鲁棒性和可重构性。

Conclusion: 该图论框架确立了网络复杂性作为实现高容量、可重构拓扑绝缘体的资源，将拓扑物态与复杂网络研究进一步融合。

Abstract: The landscape of topological insulators has expanded beyond its traditional domain of periodic lattices with short-range hopping. Related studies have explored topological phenomena under non-Euclidean geometries, disordered structures, and long-range hopping, progressively narrowing the gap between topological phases of matter and complex networks. Here we demonstrate that genuine complex networks, far beyond periodic lattices and their conventional variants, can themselves host topological insulating phases. By developing a nonconflicting design framework for multiple topological objectives, we realize topological insulators in high-degree Hofstadter lattices and across regular, small-world, and random networks. This generalization uncovers distinct network-specific features, including an expanded range of attainable topological characteristics, a degree-dependent transition from Hofstadter-type to Haldane-type Chern insulators, and, most notably, the superior robustness and reconfigurability of small-world topological insulators. Our graph-theoretic framework establishes network complexity as a resource for realizing high-capacity and reconfigurable topological insulators.

</details>


### [135] [Evaluating Chern Topology in Discretized Brillouin Zones Using Bargmann Invariants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03104)
*Swarup Sangiri,A Taraphder*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 提出基于三阶Bargmann不变量的有限态几何框架，用规范不变的态重叠在离散参数空间直接计算陈数并刻画局域量子态几何，并在多个典型拓扑模型中验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统陈数计算通常依赖贝里曲率的连续积分或动力学演化，需要一个能直接从有限态数据出发、同时保留局域几何信息且不依赖动力学演化的紧凑描述框架。

Method: 以三阶Bargmann不变量（规范不变态重叠）为核心构建有限态几何框架；在离散化参数空间上直接求陈数；通过相位导出密度与互补振幅密度保留局域几何信息，并提取拓扑性质变化的特征信号；对隔离复合子空间做rank-2扩展以处理复合子空间拓扑。

Result: 该框架成功应用于Qi-Wu-Zhang模型、1/3与1/4通量Hofstadter模型、SSH模型与Rice-Mele模型，并覆盖闭环绕与参数空间拓扑情形，实现由有限态数据直接组织陈拓扑与局域几何信息。

Conclusion: 三阶Bargmann不变量框架提供了一种不依赖动力学演化的态基紧凑描述，可统一处理陈拓扑与局域量子态几何，并具备向复合子空间和不同拓扑模型推广的能力。

Abstract: We develop a finite-state geometric framework based on third-order Bargmann invariants for evaluating Chern numbers and characterizing local quantum-state geometry. Using gauge-invariant state overlaps, the framework evaluates the Chern number directly on discretized parameter spaces while retaining local geometric information through the phase-derived density and the complementary amplitude density, as well as signatures associated with changes in topological character. We apply the construction to the Qi--Wu--Zhang, $1/3$- and $1/4$-flux Hofstadter, SSH, and Rice--Mele models, including a rank-$2$ extension for isolated composite subspaces and applications to closed-loop and parameter-space topology. The resulting framework organizes Chern topology and local geometric information directly from finite-state data, providing a compact state-based description without relying on dynamical evolution.

</details>


### [136] [Plasmon modes in tilted three-dimensional nodal-ring semimetals. II. Vortex nodal ring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03188)
*Ipsita Mandal*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文研究三维涡旋节点环半金属（VNR）中的集体电荷激发，探讨其赝自旋织构如何改变等离激元响应，并与常规PT对称节点环半金属（PTNR）对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VNR的赝自旋织构绕节点环缠绕，与PTNR定性不同；尽管两者线性化能带色散相同，织构差异可能带来超出能带色散的额外影响，需澄清赝自旋织构对集体电荷动力学的作用。

Method: 采用低能两带描述与随机相位近似（RPA），分析低掺杂下的密度响应、态密度、Drude权重、介电函数和等离激元模式；考虑未倾斜体系以及面内和轴向能带倾斜情形。

Result: VNR与PTNR虽哈密顿量、赝自旋织构和贝里曲率分布不同，线性化能带色散却一致；涡旋织构在带交叠因子和量子度规中引入环向角依赖，从而修正电荷响应，并影响介电屏蔽与不同波矢取向下的集体模式。

Conclusion: 结果表明，除能带色散外，赝自旋织构本身也能显著影响三维节点环半金属的集体电荷动力学。

Abstract: We investigate collective charge excitations in three-dimensional vortex nodal-ring semimetals (VNRs), whose pseudospin texture winds around the nodal ring and differs qualitatively from that of the conventional $\mathcal{PT}$-symmetric nodal-ring semimetal (PTNR). While plasmon modes in PTNRs have been studied in a companion work, arXiv:2609.35373. Here, we focus on how a VNR's pseudospin texture changes the response compared with the former. Within a low-energy two-band description and the random-phase approximation, we analyse the density response, density-of-states, Drude weights, dielectric function, and plasmon modes at low doping. We consider the untilted system together with in-plane and axial band-tilts. Although the VNR and PTNR have distinct Hamiltonians, pseudospin textures, and Berry-curvature distributions, their linearised energy dispersions coincide. The vortex texture, however, introduces a toroidal-angle dependence in the band-overlap factor and quantum metric, thereby modifying the charge response. We examine how these effects enter the dielectric screening and collective modes for different wavevector orientations. Our results clarify how pseudospin texture, beyond the band dispersion alone, influences collective charge dynamics in three-dimensional nodal-ring semimetals.

</details>


### [137] [Beyond Pure Dephasing: Quantum Error Correction in Single Molecules Requires Multiple Spins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03318)
*Matteo Mezzadri,Silvia Macedonio,Luca Lepori,Emilio Macaluso,Francesco Albarelli,Richard E. P. Winpenny,Alessandro Chiesa,Stefano Carretta1*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文提出以多自旋分子作为容错量子计算架构，采用混合编码纠错协议同时处理对角（退相位）与非对角（弛豫）误差，并通过数值模拟验证其性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分子自旋具有不同误差之间的强层级关系以及可在合成层面工程化多自旋分子的特性，但非谐系统在非对角误差下相干性损失会阻碍单自旋层面的纠错，因此需要新的容错架构与纠错方案。

Method: 提出一种处理对角与非对角误差的纠错协议，基于混合编码：将抗退相位单元（抑制主导的纯退相位误差）与实现多量子比特码（针对残余非对角误差）的多自旋分子相结合，利用分子自旋的强误差层级与合成可工程化特性。

Result: 借助第一级编码对退相位的巨大抑制，数值证明该策略在每逻辑单元仅含有限自旋数时仍具潜在性能优势。

Conclusion: 多自旋分子结合混合编码纠错是实现容错量子计算的可行架构，可在有限自旋资源下有效抑制误差，解决非谐系统非对角误差难以单自旋纠正的问题。

Abstract: We propose multi-spin molecules as a viable architecture for fault-tolerant quantum computing. To this aim, we introduce a correction protocol handling both diagonal and off-diagonal errors, typically associated with dephasing and relaxation. The scheme is based on a hybrid encoding which combines dephasing-tolerant units suppressing the leading pure dephasing error into a multi-spin molecule implementing a multi-qubit code for residual off-diagonal errors. Our proposal leverages peculiar properties of molecular spins, i.e. the strong hierarchy between different errors and the possibility to engineer multi-spin molecules at the synthetic level. Moreover, it addresses the important issue of the loss of coherences in anharmonic systems subject to off-diagonal errors, which hampers their correction at the single spin level. Thanks to the huge suppression of dephasing by the first-level code, we numerically demonstrate the potential performance of this strategy even with a limited number of spins per logical unit.

</details>


### [138] [Complete Magnetic Hierarchy in Bichromatically Driven Unconventional Magnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03461)
*Pei-Hao Fu,Sayed Ali Akbar Ghorash*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文建立双色驱动下非常规磁性工程的通用框架，揭示连续q波磁体与晶格高梯度项共同构成的完备磁层级，并在d波交替磁体上验证驱动不对称性向光缀饰电子结构的转移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单频驱动与静态条件下对非常规磁性的调控存在局限，特别是难以生成混合宇称的磁层级以及动量不对称的能带结构，因此需要一种更一般的驱动方案来系统性地工程化非常规磁性。

Method: 采用双色（bichromatic）Floquet驱动，考察具有一般偶公度频率比的双色驱动；分析连续q波磁体的领先项贡献，并引入晶格高梯度项补足缺失的宇称阶；以圆-线偏振光辐照的d波交替磁体为具体模型进行验证；并推广至高阶非常规磁体。

Result: 对频率比为偶公度比的双色驱动，连续q波磁体仅生成到q-2阶分量，而晶格高梯度项提供缺失的q-1与q宇称阶，从而完成到q阶的完整层级；在d波交替磁体上，驱动场的不对称性转移至光缀饰电子结构，产生超越静态与单频极限的动量不对称色散；该能带不对称性在领先连续展开中缺失，源于高梯度晶格项被双色驱动转化为动量奇次贡献；并得到所有q宇称非常规磁体的完整磁层级。

Conclusion: 双色Floquet驱动可作为生成混合宇称磁层级与动态工程化非常规磁性的通用途径。

Abstract: We establish a general framework for engineering unconventional magnetism using bichromatic driving. We identify a sharp hierarchy: for a bichromatic drive with a generic even commensurate frequency ratio, the leading continuum $q$-wave magnet generates components only up to order $q-2$, whereas lattice higher-gradient terms supply the missing $q-1$ and $q$ parities and complete the hierarchy through order $q$. We explicitly illustrate this new revised hierarchy on a $d$-wave altermagnet irradiated by a circularly-linearly polarized light, and show that the asymmetry of the driving field is transferred to the light-dressed electronic structure, producing momentum-asymmetric dispersions beyond the static and single-frequency limits. We further show that this band asymmetry is absent in the leading continuum expansion and originates from higher-gradient lattice terms that are converted by the bichromatic drive into odd-in-momentum contributions. Extending the analysis to higher-order unconventional magnets, we further obtain complete magnetic hierarchies for all $q$-parity unconventional magnets. Our results establish bichromatic Floquet driving as a general route for generating mixed-parity magnetic hierarchies and dynamically engineering unconventional magnetism.

</details>


### [139] [Electrostatic Doping of Moiré Superlattices Controls the Optical Fingerprint of a WSe_2 /Twisted Bilayer Graphene heterostructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03532)
*Houssem Eddine Hannachi,Sihem Jaziri*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文理论研究了静电掺杂下、垂直堆叠于扭转双层石墨烯(tBG)上的WSe2单层的光学响应，揭示掺杂莫尔超晶格产生的空间调制静电势通过屏蔽库仑作用耦合激子电子-空穴成分，导致激子吸收谱出现多重卫星共振。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索tBG中静电掺杂对邻近半导体单层激子态的调控机制，为莫尔异质结构中的激子-电荷密度耦合提供微观解释，并推动新型光学传感器件的设计。

Method: 针对扭转角低于魔角(1.1°)、电子关联显著增强的体系，构建WSe2/tBG异质结构模型，考察掺杂莫尔超晶格的空间调制静电势经屏蔽库仑相互作用与WSe2激子的电子、空穴成分耦合；分析激子质心与内禀自由度的相互影响，以及掺杂诱导的不同轨道对称性里德伯激子态之间的杂化。

Result: 计算表明静电掺杂引起激子吸收谱显著改变，出现多个卫星共振；这些谱特征源于激子质心与内禀自由度的耦合以及不同轨道对称性里德伯态间的掺杂诱导杂化。计算结果与此前实验观测和理论预测吻合良好。

Conclusion: tBG中的静电掺杂可有效调控相邻半导体单层的激子态，为新型光学传感器件提供可行途径。

Abstract: We theoretically investigate the optical response of the WSe2 monolayer vertically stacked on twisted bilayer graphene (tBG) under electrostatic doping. In this heterostructure, the doped moiré superlattice of tBG generates a spatially modulated electrostatic potential that couples to the electron and hole constituents of the WSe2 exciton through the screened Coulomb interaction, thereby providing a microscopic mechanism for exciton coupling to the charge-density landscape of the moiré Fermi sea. Focusing on twist angles below the magic angle (1.1°), where electronic correlations are strongly enhanced, we demonstrate that electrostatic doping induces pronounced modifications of the excitonic absorption spectrum, including the emergence of multiple satellite resonances. We show that these spectral features originate from the interplay between the exciton center-of-mass and internal degrees of freedom, together with doping-induced hybridization between Rydberg exciton states of different orbital symmetry. The calculated spectra are in good agreement with previously reported experimental observations and theoretical predictions. Our results establish electrostatic doping in tBG as an effective means of engineering excitonic states in adjacent semiconducting monolayers and provide a pathway toward novel optical sensor devices.

</details>

### [140] [Projective symmetry group classification of fermionic $Z_2$ spin liquids on the dipolar-octupolar pyrochlore magnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02409)
*Rohit Deb,Sambuddha Sanyal*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文在偶极-八极（DO）烧绿石格子上构造并分类了费米子部分子的 Z2 量子自旋液体（QSL），发现 DO 对称性作用选取了物理上不同的对称允许平均场通道，导致 ansatz、自旋子谱和可观测量均不同于有效自旋-1/2 情形和玻色子 DO 分类；自洽计算给出 (Jx, Jz) 平面相图，仅少数 Z2 态稳定，并计算了自旋子能带、低温比热和动力学自旋结构因子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Z2 拓扑序在费米子和玻色子自旋子图像中都能自然实现，因此需要把 DO 烧绿石 QSL 的费米子部分子分类从 U(1) 扇区推广到 Z2 扇区，并厘清 DO 对称性下费米子图像与有效自旋-1/2 及玻色子 DO 分类的物理差异，以为 Nd2ScNbO7 等 DO 材料的无能隙分数化热力学提供费米子部分子解释。

Method: 在 DO 烧绿石格子上构造费米子部分子的 Z2 QSL；利用投影对称群（PSG）分类，得到与一般费米子分类相同的 48 个对称 Z2 类；以一组与子晶格无关的算符编码 DO 对称性作用，筛选对称允许的平均场通道；将各向异性 XYZ 交换模型分解为所有对称的单重态与三重态跃迁及配对通道，并自洽最小化平均场能量，在 (Jx, Jz) 平面绘制相图；对稳定态计算自旋子能带结构、低温比热和动力学自旋结构因子。

Result: PSG 给出与一般费米子分类相同的 48 个对称 Z2 类，但 DO 的独特对称性作用选择出物理上不同的对称允许平均场通道，所得 ansatz、自旋子谱和可观测量不同于有效自旋-1/2 情形与玻色子 DO 分类。相图中仅少数 Z2 态稳定，其中若干支持无能隙或平带自旋子谱。计算的低温比热幂律指数可作为自旋子节点结构的诊断量；近线性温度比热配合结构因子中的宽连续谱，为 Nd2ScNbO7 等 DO 材料报道的无能隙分数化热力学提供了费米子部分子解释，补充了 Ce 基化合物的玻色子量子自旋冰图像。

Conclusion: 该工作将 DO 烧绿石 QSL 的费米子部分子分类推广到 U(1) 扇区之外，在 Z2 拓扑序中费米子与玻色子自旋子同样自然地实现；费米子部分子图像能解释 DO 材料中观察到的无能隙分数化热力学。

Abstract: We construct and classify $Z_2$ quantum spin liquids (QSLs) of fermionic partons on the dipolar-octupolar (DO) pyrochlore lattice. The DO pseudospin shares the space-group and time-reversal algebra of the effective spin-1/2 Kramers doublet, its projective symmetry group (PSG) yields the same 48 symmetric $Z_2$ classes as the generic fermionic classification. We show, the distinct DO symmetry action, encoded in a set of sublattice-independent operators, selects a physically different set of symmetry-allowed mean-field channels, so that the resulting ansatze, spinon spectra, and observables differ from both the effective spin-1/2 case and the bosonic DO classification. Decomposing the anisotropic XYZ exchange model into all symmetric singlet and triplet hopping and pairing channels and minimizing the mean-field energy self-consistently, we obtain phase diagrams in the ($\tilde{J_x}, \tilde{J_z}$) plane in which only a few $Z_2$ states, several supporting gapless or flat-band spinon spectra, are stabilized. For these states we compute the spinon band structures, the low-temperature specific heat, and the dynamical spin structure factor, and we argue that the power-law specific-heat exponent is a diagnostic of the spinon nodal structure. A near-linear-in-temperature specific heat together with broad continua in the structure factor gives a fermionic-parton account of the gapless, fractionalized thermodynamics reported in DO materials such as $Nd_2ScNbO_7$, complementing the bosonic quantum-spin-ice picture of the Ce-based compounds. The work extends the fermionic-parton classification of DO pyrochlore QSLs beyond the $U(1)$ sector, where $Z_2$ topological order is realized equally naturally by fermionic and bosonic spinons.

</details>


### [141] [Fractionalization-Induced Time-Reversal Symmetry Breaking at Continuous Superconducting Transitions in Low-Symmetry Kondo Lattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02635)
*Wonjune Choi,Shi-Zeng Lin*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 对称性分数化可实现超导与额外对称性破缺的单次连续转变，无需多维序参量或精细调节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统朗道理论中，超导与额外对称性的同时连续出现通常需要多维序参量或精细调节，本文寻找新的机制路径。

Method: 在具有 \mathbb Z_2 自旋液体的近藤晶格中，采用投影对称性分析；以各向异性 Kondo-Kitaev 模型作为具体实现。

Result: 当投影时间反演作用在这些场上的平方为 -1 时，投影对称代数禁止保持时间反演对称的凝聚，并强制临界模二重简并；近藤凝聚同时破坏电磁 U(1) 对称性，可实现超导、时间反演与镜像对称性同时破缺的单一连续相变。

Conclusion: 对称性分数化为超导与附加对称性同时连续破缺提供了超越传统朗道范式的机制，且在晶体对称性仅允许一维不可约表示时依然成立。

Abstract: In conventional Landau theory, simultaneous continuous onset of superconductivity and the breaking of additional symmetries generally requires a multidimensional order parameter or fine tuning. We show that symmetry fractionalization provides a different route. In a Kondo lattice with a $\mathbb Z_2$ spin liquid, the Kondo fields inherit the projective symmetry action of the spinons. When the projective time-reversal action on these fields squares to $-1$, the projective symmetry algebra forbids a condensate that preserves time-reversal symmetry and enforces twofold degeneracy of the critical modes. Because Kondo condensation in a $\mathbb Z_2$ spin liquid also breaks electromagnetic $U(1)$ symmetry, superconductivity and time-reversal symmetry breaking can emerge at a single continuous transition, even if crystal symmetry admits only one-dimensional irreducible representations. We demonstrate this mechanism in an anisotropic Kondo-Kitaev model, where superconductivity and the breaking of time-reversal and mirror symmetries emerge simultaneously.

</details>


### [142] [Correlated Metals, Metamagnetism, and Orbital Nematic Order in moiré Materials with Neural Quantum States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03145)
*Wei Zhang,Ataç İmamoğlu,Tao Shi,Eugene A. Demler,Ivan Morera*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该工作利用基于消息传递神经网络的神经量子态与变分蒙特卡洛方法，研究了填充因子ν=2下受三角周期势与长程库仑相互作用调控的二维电子系统，给出了随周期势强度和塞曼场变化的基态相图，揭示了强关联导致的金属-绝缘体转变及其对自旋极化的强烈依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非相互作用情形下系统本应形成常规能带绝缘体，但强关联是否以及如何重塑甚至压制能带绝缘态、产生关联金属相尚不清楚；同时金属-绝缘体转变对自旋极化的依赖、以及长程库仑相互作用的具体作用也缺乏系统研究。

Method: 采用基于消息传递神经网络的神经量子态表示波函数，结合变分蒙特卡洛方法进行优化，在ν=2填充因子下求解受长程库仑相互作用与三角周期势作用的二维电子连续模型，并扫描周期势强度与塞曼场；此外还对比研究短程排斥相互作用以厘清库仑长程性的作用。

Result: 强关联可定性重塑绝缘态，在部分相图中压制能带绝缘体而形成关联金属相，二者之间为一阶转变。金属-绝缘体转变强烈依赖自旋极化：未极化系统在较弱周期势下即通过形成s波分子绝缘体而绝缘化，其中自旋相反的电子在势阱极小值附近成键；而完全极化系统需要显著更深的势阱，并形成对称性破缺的轨道向列分子晶体。这种自旋依赖的分子转变导致磁化曲线不连续、变磁转变以及相图中的多个多临界点，并在密度分布、实空间关联函数和结构因子中留下明显特征。将长程库仑作用替换为短程排斥作用后，多体相图的定性结构保持不变，表明结果对不同相互作用模型具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 强关联效应可在周期势中显著重构电子绝缘态，并产生具有自旋选择性的分子绝缘体与分子晶体相及丰富的多临界行为；这些结论在长程库仑与短程排斥相互作用下均成立，具有较好的鲁棒性，并可在超冷原子平台上实现与检验。

Abstract: In this work, we investigate a continuum model of two-dimensional electrons interacting via long-range Coulomb forces and subject to a triangular periodic potential at filling factor $ν=2$. Using neural quantum states based on message-passing neural networks optimized with variational Monte Carlo, we determine the ground-state phase diagram as a function of periodic potential strength and Zeeman field. Although the noninteracting system is expected to form a conventional band insulator, we show that strong correlations can qualitatively reshape the insulating state and, in part of the phase diagram, suppress the band insulator in favor of a correlated metallic phase, with a first-order transition separating the two regimes. Moreover, the metal--insulator transition exhibits a strong dependence on spin polarization: the unpolarized system becomes insulating at substantially weaker periodic potentials through the formation of an $s$-wave molecular insulator, where opposite-spin electrons bind near each potential minimum, whereas the fully polarized system requires a significantly deeper potential and instead forms a symmetry-broken orbital nematic molecular crystal. This spin-dependent molecular transition gives rise to discontinuous magnetization curves, metamagnetic transitions, and several multicritical points in the phase diagram, together with clear signatures in the density profiles, real-space correlation functions, and structure factors. To further elucidate the role of the long-range character of the Coulomb interaction, we also study short-range repulsive interactions, which can be realized with ultracold-atom platforms. We find that the qualitative structure of the many-body phase diagram remains unchanged, demonstrating the robustness of our results across different interaction models.

</details>


### [143] [Melting phase diagram of the two-dimensional electron solid in a perpendicular magnetic field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03513)
*M. Yu. Melnikov,D. G. Smirnov,A. A. Shashkin,S. -H. Huang,C. ~W. Liu,S. V. Kravchenko*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 在超高迁移率SiGe/Si/SiGe量子阱二维电子系统中，通过测量绝缘态双阈值伏安曲线起始密度，确定了电子固体的热熔化相图；磁场使起始密度显著增大，熔化温度随磁场升高，并在固定磁场下随电子密度呈现平台，可用微晶中的共振模式解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究磁场对二维电子固体热熔化行为的影响，并检验其与理论预测的一致性，同时探究熔化温度随电子密度的依赖关系。

Method: 在垂直磁场和不同温度下，对超高迁移率SiGe/Si/SiGe量子阱二维电子系统绝缘态的双阈值电压-电流曲线进行测量，以确定电子固体的起始密度，从而构建热熔化相图。

Result: 磁场引入使起始密度显著增大，该效应在最低温度最强并随温度升高而减弱；熔化温度随磁场增加而升高。数据与理论预测一致。意外的是，在固定磁场下熔化温度随电子密度出现平台。

Conclusion: 实验结果支持电子固体热熔化的理论预测，并揭示了固定磁场下熔化温度随电子密度的平台现象，该现象可归因于微晶中的共振模式。

Abstract: We determine the thermal melting phase diagram of the electron solid by measuring the onset density of the double-threshold voltage-current curves in the insulating state in the ultra-high mobility two-dimensional electron system in SiGe/Si/SiGe quantum wells at different temperatures in perpendicular magnetic fields. The introduction of a magnetic field leads to a considerable increase in the onset density, which is strongest at the lowest temperatures and decreases with increasing temperature, and the melting temperature increases with magnetic field. These data are in agreement with theoretical predictions. Unexpectedly, the melting temperature as a function of electron density in a fixed magnetic field reveals a plateau, which can be interpreted in terms of resonance modes in the crystallites.

</details>


### [144] [Consistent perturbation expansions in screened interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03569)
*Abdallah Arthur El Sahili,Francesco Sottile,Lucia Reining*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出了若干原则上精确的多体微扰展开，并讨论了如何一致地使用它们，重点关注前两阶屏蔽库仑相互作用及围绕Kohn-Sham体系的一致展开，并在对称Hubbard二聚体上验证其性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多体微扰理论虽能计算光谱量与总能量，但不同近似方案导致结果差异很大，因此需要建立原则上精确且可一致使用的展开框架。

Method: 提出多种原则上精确的展开式并讨论其一致使用方法，聚焦于相应屏蔽库仑相互作用的前两阶，特别是围绕Kohn-Sham体系的一致展开；使用对称Hubbard二聚体在不同关联强度下评估各方案表现。

Result: 基于精确Kohn-Sham组分的展开在能量和光谱方面表现突出，是通向良好能量与良好光谱的有前景途径。

Conclusion: 基于精确Kohn-Sham组分的一致展开为同时获得良好能量与光谱提供了一条有前景的路线。

Abstract: Many-body perturbation theory is a powerful framework to calculate spectroscopic quantities, as well as the total energy. However, the field suffers from the fact that different flavors of approximations lead to a wide range of results. We present several in principle exact expansions and discuss how to use them consistently. We concentrate on the first two orders in the respective screened Coulomb interaction. Of special interest is a consistent expansion around the Kohn-Sham system. We illustrate the performance of the various choices using the symmetric Hubbard dimer over a wide range of correlation strength. In particular, the expansion based on exact Kohn-Sham ingredients stands out as a promising route towards good energies and good spectra.

</details>


### [145] [Decohering Kitaev's Sixteenfold Way: A Holographic Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03610)
*Shuyu Zhang,Linhao Li,Zhen Bi,Tzu-Chieh Wei,Zijian Song*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究二维拓扑序中的任意子退相干，以Kitaev十六重态中的费米子退相干为显例，提出退相干诱导的相变密度矩阵可通过"提升到Walker-Wang模型→转变为另一Walker-Wang模型→对体自由度求迹"三步获得，从而将二维拓扑序的退相干与三维对应物的纯态相变联系起来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 十六重态包含手征拓扑相，这类相被认为不存在对易投影算符哈密顿量，因此难以直接研究其退相干。作者希望找到一种系统方法，在保持对易投影算符哈密顿量框架的前提下研究任意子退相干，特别是手征拓扑序中的退相干。

Method: 以Kitaev十六重态中的费米子退相干为具体例子研究二维拓扑序的任意子退相干；提出三步构造猜想：首先将拓扑序提升到其Walker-Wang模型，然后转变到另一个Walker-Wang模型，最后对体自由度求迹，从而得到退相干诱导相变的密度矩阵，并给出多个例子验证。

Result: 证明十六重态中每一个态对费米子退相干都产生内禀混合态拓扑序，其根源在于对应的三维体总是转变到三维费米子toric code (fTC)。同时给出多个示例说明该三步构造的有效性。

Conclusion: 该结果将二维拓扑序的退相干与其三维对应物的纯态相变联系起来，为在手征拓扑序中于对易投影算符哈密顿量层面系统研究任意子退相干提供了方法。

Abstract: We study anyon decoherence in 2D topological order, with the fermion decoherence in Kitaev's sixteenfold way as explicit examples. The sixteenfold way includes chiral topological phases, which are believed to admit no commuting projector Hamiltonian, making it challenging to study their decoherence directly. We further conjecture the density matrix produced by this decoherence induced phase transition can be obtained by a three-step procedure: first lift the topological order to its Walker-Wang model, followed by a transition to another Walker-Wang model, and finally trace over the bulk degrees of freedom. This establishes a connection between the decoherence of a topological order and a pure state phase transition in its 3D counterpart. Many examples are given. In particular, we show that decohering the fermion in each of the sixteen states always yields an intrinsically mixed-state topological order, a consequence of the fact the corresponding 3D bulk always transits into the 3D fermionic toric code (fTC). Our result suggests a systematic method for studying anyon decoherence in chiral topological orders at the level of commuting projector Hamiltonians.

</details>


### [146] [Finite-momentum pairing and magnetic halos in a spin-imbalanced Holstein model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03678)
*Chunhan Feng,George Batrouni,Richard Scalettar*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文证明Holstein模型通过声子延迟吸引有效抑制自旋失衡下的符号涨落，使行列式量子蒙特卡洛可深入研究二维FFLO超导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维FFLO超导（自旋失衡驱动的有限动量库珀配对）数值研究困难：自旋失衡吸引Hubbard模型存在费米子符号问题，量子蒙特卡洛难以达到配对关联最强的低温区。

Method: 采用Holstein模型，其中有效吸引由声子动力学延迟，从而在自旋失衡下强烈抑制符号涨落，实现无偏的行列式量子蒙特卡洛模拟；通过实空间配对关联、配对磁化率随动量Q的变化以及电荷/自旋关联进行表征。

Result: 随磁化增强，零动量配对演化为有限动量FFLO配对，表现为实空间配对关联振荡和配对磁化率峰移至有限Q；有限动量磁化率显著高于零动量值。高密度n~0.875、磁化m≈1-n（多数自旋密度接近半满）时，FFLO配对与短程棋盘电荷密度波序共存，并在较大距离出现交错纵向自旋关联构成的磁性晕。

Conclusion: 这些空间纹理为有限动量配对提供了可实验探测的特征信号，并推动冷原子体系实现FFLO物理。

Abstract: Fulde-Ferrell-Larkin-Ovchinnikov (FFLO) superconductivity, Cooper pairing at finite-momentum driven by spin imbalance, remains difficult to study numerically in two dimensions. The fermion sign problem in the spin-imbalanced attractive Hubbard model severely limits quantum Monte Carlo access to the low temperatures where pairing correlations are most pronounced. We show that the Holstein model, in which the effective attraction is retarded by phonon dynamics, strongly suppresses sign fluctuations under spin imbalance, enabling unbiased determinant quantum Monte Carlo simulations deep in this regime. With increasing magnetization, zero-momentum pairing evolves into finite-momentum FFLO pairing, evidenced by oscillating real-space pair correlations and a shift of the pairing susceptibility peak to finite $\vec{Q}$. The finite-momentum susceptibility substantially exceeds its zero-momentum value. At high density $n \sim 0.875$, near magnetization $m \approx 1-n$ where the majority-spin density approaches half filling, FFLO pairing coexists with checkerboard charge-density-wave order at short range, while staggered longitudinal spin correlations emerge as a magnetic halo at larger distances. These spatial textures provide experimentally accessible signatures of finite-momentum pairing and motivate cold-atom realizations of FFLO physics.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.quant-gas'></div>

### [147] [Spectral Crossings Diagnose Multiply Quantized Vortex Splitting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02234)
*Jongheon Baek,Hiroyuki Tajima,Gihwan Nam*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文研究电荷-r耦合涡旋在弱对称破缺后保持融合零模或分裂为r个单元电荷零模的两种构型，通过双组分Haldane球模型加Raman场，揭示了二者的零能穿越失谐标度律差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 融合的order-r零模与分裂的r个单元电荷零模具有相同的总绕数和相同的朗道能级投影暗态数目，因此施加的轨道角动量和总绕数均无法区分其局域几何结构，需要寻找新的区分手段。

Method: 构建双组分Haldane球模型并用Raman场耦合；对单极磁通N，推导缺陷分支穿越零能的失谐τ_c标度律；采用Feshbach约化将结果推广到完整多带哈密顿量；在单极-谐振子基下做数值计算。

Result: 融合零模的零能穿越失谐标度为|τ_c|~N^{-(r+1)}，而分裂的单元零模为N^{-2}；Feshbach约化对完整多带哈密顿量建立了这些渐近律，数值计算在r=1,2,3时证实了融合涡旋的标度行为；对于直径为d的部分分裂簇，当d与磁长度ℓ_B相当时标度发生交叉过渡。

Conclusion: 标度律差异可用于通过光谱推断分裂簇的直径d，即使d低于直接成像的空间分辨率也可分辨，从而为区分这两种几何构型提供光谱学手段。

Abstract: A charge-$r$ coupling vortex can remain as one fused order-$r$ zero or split into $r$ nearby unit-charge zeros after weak symmetry breaking. The two configurations have the same total winding and the same number of dark states in the lowest-Landau-level projection, so neither the imposed orbital angular momentum nor the total winding number distinguishes their local geometry. We study this distinction in a two-component Haldane-sphere model coupled by a Raman field. For monopole flux $N$, the detuning $τ_{\rm c}$ at which a defect branch crosses zero energy scales as $|τ_{\rm c}|\sim N^{-(r+1)}$ for a fused zero and $N^{-2}$ for resolved unit zeros. A Feshbach reduction establishes these asymptotic laws for the full multiband Hamiltonian, and numerical calculations in a monopole-harmonic basis confirm the fused-vortex scaling for $r=1,2,3$. For a partially split cluster of diameter $d$, the scaling crosses over when $d$ is comparable to the magnetic length $\ell_B$, allowing $d$ to be inferred spectroscopically even below the spatial resolution of direct imaging.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.stat-mech'></div>

# cond-mat.stat-mech [[Back]](#toc)

### [148] [Structural crossover of complex networks: bridging degree correlation and fractality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02883)
*Shogo Mizutaka,Jun Yamamoto,Kousuke Yakubo*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究无标度随机网络中长期度相关性与分形性之间的关系，通过分析渗流阈值处的度相关性，推导出球体积表达式，揭示了依赖度的结构交叉现象，并从临界分支过程角度解释该交叉，且在实证分形网络中观察到。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究无标度随机网络中长程度相关性与分形性之间的内在联系，特别是渗流阈值处网络的几何与拓扑特性如何相互影响。

Method: 在渗流阈值（巨连通分支出现处）分析无标度随机网络的度相关性，推导以度为k的根节点为起点、距离l内的平均节点数（球体积）表达式，并结合临界分支过程理论进行分析。

Result: 球体积表达式预测了依赖度的结构交叉：当l远小于k^{1/(df-1)}时，球体积按~kl增长；当l远大于该尺度时，过渡到全局分形标度~l^{df}（df为临界无标度随机网络的分形维数）。该交叉在实证分形网络中也被观察到。

Conclusion: 结构交叉可自然地从临界分支过程角度理解，且该现象在真实分形网络中同样存在，表明长程度相关性与分形性之间存在深刻联系。

Abstract: We investigate the relationship between long-range degree correlations and fractality in scale-free random networks. By analyzing degree correlations in scale-free random networks at the percolation threshold, at which a giant component emerges, we derive the ball volume $\tildeν_k(l)$, defined as the average number of nodes within distance $l$ from a root node with degree $k$. The resulting expression predicts a degree-dependent structural crossover. For $l\ll k^{1/(\df-1)}$, the ball volume grows as $\tildeν_k(l)\sim kl$, whereas for $l\gg k^{1/(\df-1)}$, it crosses over to the global fractal scaling $\tildeν_k(l)\sim l^{\df}$. Here, $\df$ is the fractal dimension of critical scale-free random networks. We further show that the structural crossover can be naturally understood in terms of critical branching processes and is also observed in empirical fractal networks.

</details>


### [149] [Probing fluctuating hydrodynamics through current fluctuations in hard rod gas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03180)
*Tirthankar Mondal,Indranil Mukherjee,Anupam Kundu*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究一维硬杆气体中积分电流的全计数统计，比较BMFT与CBFHD两种涨落流体力学框架的预测，并与微观动力学数值模拟对照。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硬杆气体是研究一维弹道输运与涨落流体力学的典型模型，而新提出的BMFT与CBFHD框架对电流涨落的预测能力尚缺乏系统检验，需明确哪种理论能准确刻画大尺度电流涨落。

Method: 在平衡态下研究积分电流的全计数统计，分别采用弹道宏观涨落理论（BMFT）和基于硬杆准粒子关联布朗运动近似的扩散型涨落流体力学（CBFHD）；在BMFT中导出弹道标度下的大偏差函数与电流累积量表达式，在CBFHD中引入随机噪声与耗散项并证明大偏差原理；随后用微观硬杆动力学的直接数值模拟及关联布朗运动集合验证。

Result: BMFT预测前几阶累积量与速率函数与微观硬杆动力学数值模拟高度一致；CBFHD的相应预测与微观模拟不符，但可由关联布朗运动集合独立复现，说明CBFHD的偏差源于其近似本身。

Conclusion: CBFHD无法准确刻画硬杆气体的大尺度电流涨落，而BMFT至少在积分电流全计数统计方面与微观动力学定量符合。

Abstract: We study the full counting statistics of the integrated current in a one-dimensional hard-rod gas in equilibrium, employing two recently proposed fluctuating hydrodynamic frameworks: (i) Ballistic Macroscopic Fluctuation Theory (BMFT) and (ii) diffusive fluctuating hydrodynamic theory based on correlated Brownian motion approximation of hard-rod quasiparticles (CBFHD). We investigate how the predictions of these two approaches compare with each other and with the underlying microscopic dynamics. We show within BMFT that the current distribution admits a large-deviation form under ballistic scaling, and we derive an explicit expression for the large-deviation function, valid for an arbitrary inhomogeneous initial state factorized in point-particle coordinates. The corresponding current cumulants are also obtained. We subsequently analyze the current statistics within the CBFHD framework, which explicitly incorporates stochastic noise and dissipative terms, and demonstrate that the current distribution likewise obeys a large-deviation principle in this approach. We find that, while BMFT predictions for the first few cumulants and the rate function are in excellent agreement with direct numerical simulations of the microscopic hard-rod dynamics, CBFHD prediction is not. We further verify the predictions of the CBFHD independently using a collection of correlated Brownian motions, which provides an approximate description of the dynamics of hard-rod quasiparticles. Our findings indicate that CBFHD does not accurately capture large-scale current fluctuations in the hard-rod gas, whereas BMFT yields predictions in quantitative agreement with the microscopic dynamics, at least in the context of full counting statistics.

</details>


### [150] [Functional renormalization group in an external field: constant field versus constant order parameter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03365)
*Julia von Rothkirch,Andreas Rückriegel,Peter Kopietz*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文在泛函重整化群框架下实现了两类边界条件（固定外场与固定共轭序参量），推导并比较了φ⁴理论的流方程，发现与相关三点顶点线性化流相关的标度变量与指数依赖于所选边界条件；并讨论了弹性对Ising临界性的影响，指出在恒定应力下重整化Ising不动点与球面不动点被移至物理上不可及的参数区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受外场作用系统的重整化群流可通过固定外场或固定共轭序参量两种方式导出，但二者在泛函重整化群中的具体实现及其对标度行为的影响尚未被系统比较；此外弹性耦合对Ising临界性的作用也需澄清。

Method: 在泛函重整化群（FRG）框架内引入并实现固定外场与固定共轭序参量两类边界条件，针对φ⁴理论推导对应的流方程并加以比较，分析Ising不动点附近相关三点顶点线性化流所涉及的标度变量与标度指数，同时考察弹性效应对Ising临界性的影响，对比恒定应变与恒定应力下的重整化群处理。

Result: 两类边界条件给出的流方程在Ising不动点附近的部分标度变量与标度指数上存在差异；在弹性耦合情形下，恒定应变得到的重整化Ising不动点与球面不动点，在改为恒定应力进行重整化群计算后被移至参数空间中物理上不可及的区域。

Conclusion: 边界条件的选取（固定外场或固定共轭序参量）会影响相关标度量与标度指数，因此在外场系统中进行泛函重整化群分析时必须明确所采用的边界条件；弹性耦合下，恒定应力与恒定应变对应不同的物理可达不动点结构。

Abstract: Renormalization group (RG) flows for systems subject to an external field can be derived either by fixing the field or by fixing the conjugate order parameter. We show how to implement these boundary conditions within the functional renormalization group approach. We derive and compare the corresponding flow equations for $φ^4$ theory and show that some scaling variables and scaling exponents associated with the linearized flow of the relevant three-point vertex in the vicinity of the Ising fixed point depend on the boundary conditions. We also discuss the effect of elasticity on Ising criticality. In this case two of the fixed points (namely the renormalized Ising fixed point and the spherical fixed point) obtained for constant strain are shifted to physically inaccessible regimes of parameter space when we perform the RG at constant stress.

</details>


### [151] [Minimax entropy production for arbitrary processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03478)
*Mahran Yousef,Ben Ansbacher,David H. Wolpert*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 研究如何在给定映射下设计系统先验分布，以最小化最坏情况实际分布下的熵产（EP）。对确定性映射给出闭式解；对随机映射证明最坏情况分布可取为单态点质量，极小极大先验近似Boltzmann-Gibbs形式并可用全局收敛算法求解，且对应极小极大失配代价等于观测输出后剩余的最大输入不确定性；连续时间情形下瞬时失配代价发散，而有限时间情形具有由最大逃逸速率决定的普适短时标度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 任何物理过程的初始状态分布变化都会改变期望熵产，而最小化特定过程EP的初始分布称为该过程的先验；改变过程的微观细节会改变先验。作者由此提出设计问题：工程师为实现给定映射设计系统时，应内置何种先验，才能在最坏情况实际分布下最小化EP。

Method: 针对确定性映射推导极小极大EP问题的闭式解；对随机映射，证明最坏情况实际分布可选取为单状态点质量，得到近似Boltzmann-Gibbs形式的极小极大先验，并提出全局收敛的数值算法；进一步将分析推广到连续时间情形。

Result: 确定性映射情形的极小极大先验有闭式解；随机映射下极小极大先验近似满足Boltzmann-Gibbs方程，可由全局收敛算法求得；对应极小极大失配代价等于观测输出后关于输入所剩余的最大不确定性；连续时间下瞬时极小极大失配代价发散，有限时间极小极大失配代价呈现由动力学最大逃逸速率设定的普适短时标度。

Conclusion: 为给定映射设计极小极大EP先验的问题在确定性与随机映射、离散与连续时间情形下均得到刻画：确定性情形有闭式解，随机情形有类Boltzmann-Gibbs最优先验与数值算法，并揭示了失配代价的信息论意义及连续时间短时标度的普适性。

Abstract: For any physical process, varying the initial distribution over its states will change the resulting expected entropy production (EP). An initial distribution that minimizes the EP of a particular process is called the prior of that process. Varying the microphysical details of the process will change its prior.We consider the following question: if an engineer wants to design a system to implement a given map, what prior should they design into the system to minimize the EP for the worst-case actual distribution? We derive the closed-form solution to this problem when the map is deterministic. We also show that for stochastic maps, a worst-case actual distribution can always be chosen to be a point mass on a single state, and that the associated minimax prior resembles a Boltzmann-Gibbs equation, and can be found by a globally convergent numerical algorithm. We also show that the associated minimax mismatch cost is the maximum uncertainty about the input that remains after observing the output. Finally, we extend the analysis to the continuous-time regime, showing that the minimax instantaneous mismatch-cost diverges, while the corresponding finite-time minimax mismatch cost has a universal short-time scaling set by the largest escape rate of the dynamics.

</details>


### [152] [Classification of topological phases of matter in stochastic systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38171)
*Dexin Li,Evelyn Tang*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文针对随机（马尔可夫）系统中的拓扑相，提出新的同伦方法与点隙重定义，建立了与马尔可夫约束一致的拓扑分类框架，发现仅无对称性与赝厄米两类对称类保持稳健，并给出非平凡相所在维度及群结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓扑相及其边态在量子系统与类比平台中已有成熟的对称性与维度分类。随机系统中也发现了拓扑相，并被推测为生化过程的机制，但缺乏与马尔可夫约束一致的分类，阻碍向新场景推广。马尔可夫约束改变矩阵空间，使既有分类方法失效，并带来新性质（如非平凡拓扑相必需非厄米性）。

Method: 引入新的同伦（homotopy）方法，并重新定义点隙（point gap），以适配马尔可夫约束下的矩阵空间，从而对随机系统中的拓扑相进行对称性分类。

Result: 在马尔可夫约束下，仅两类对称类保持稳健：无对称性与赝厄米性（pseudo-Hermiticity）。在这两类对称类中，识别出存在拓扑非平凡相的维度及其群结构。

Conclusion: 建立了可严格预测活性物质与生命物质中稳健行为的拓扑分类框架，为随机系统中拓扑相的推广提供理论基础。

Abstract: Topological phases of matter support edge states protected from noise and perturbations, and the classification of which symmetry and dimension support such phases was developed for quantum systems and analogous platforms. While topological phases have also been discovered in stochastic systems and posited as mechanisms for biochemical processes, a classification consistent with their Markovian constraints remains lacking, impeding generalization to new scenarios. These constraints alter the matrix space preventing the application of previous classification methods, while hosting new properties such as the necessity of non-Hermiticity for non-trivial topological phases. We introduce new methods including a new homotopy approach and redefinition of the point gap and find that only two symmetry classes remain robust: no symmetry and pseudo-Hermiticity. In these symmetry classes, we identify the dimensions with topologically non-trivial phases and their group structure, creating a rigorous framework for predicting robust behavior in active and living matter.

</details>


### [153] [Work fluctuation speed limit in boundary conformal field theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01248)
*Shihao Xia,Ahsan Nazir,Harry J. D. Miller*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究边界共形场论描述的量子临界系统中有限时间驱动的速度极限，推导出有限温度下弱驱动边界共形场论的精确、可饱和的涨落基速度极限，该极限完全由普适标度维数决定，并在Kibble-Zurek机制与绝热机制之间插值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究量子临界系统中有限时间驱动的根本极限，理解外部驱动产生的随机功涨落如何作为快速控制的资源，为边界临界控制建立普适的功精度-时间权衡关系。

Method: 在有限温度下研究弱驱动的边界共形场论，考虑外部驱动引起的随机功涨落，推导精确且可饱和的涨落基速度极限，并将其与饱和协议用普适标度维数表示。

Result: 得到了精确、可饱和的涨落基速度极限；该界限与饱和协议完全由普适标度维数表示，结果在时间关联强非局域的Kibble-Zurek机制与线性驱动最优的绝热机制之间插值；小标度维数时增强的时间关联导致对线性协议的显著偏离和更大的优化优势。

Conclusion: 研究确立了边界临界控制的普适功精度-时间权衡关系，可应用于量子杂质、分数量子霍尔和超导电路等平台。

Abstract: We explore the fundamental limits on finite-time driving in quantum critical systems described by boundary conformal field theory. We show that stochastic work fluctuations arising from external driving are a resource for speedy control, and derive an exact, saturable fluctuation-based speed limit in weakly driven boundary conformal field theories at finite temperature. The bound and saturating protocol can be expressed entirely in terms of the universal scaling dimension, and the result interpolates between the Kibble--Zurek regime,where temporal correlations are strongly nonlocal, and an adiabatic regime where linear driving becomes optimal. For small scaling dimension, the enhanced temporal correlations produce pronounced departures from linear protocols and a larger optimization advantage. These results establish a universal work precision--time tradeoff for boundary-critical control, applicable to quantum impurity, fractional quantum Hall, and superconducting-circuit platforms.

</details>
